So if you simply practice these things yourselves and show examples, they will learn automatically, one doesn’t have to give formal instruction in these matters.” \[Letter to Chāyā-devī dāsī, 16 February 1972\]

Therefore we have very enthusiastically met with many *mātājīs* individually or in small groups and come together in big *mātājī-saṅga* meetings in several places in Europe to discuss your teachings and quotes on these matters. We have received such a positive response that we want to continue and increase more and more.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, help us unfold your vision of a spiritual society, a house where everybody can find his or her place. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for this magnificent house. Your glories cannot be fully understood. You have done what no one else could. Now please help us all understand you by ever-increasingly unfolding your teachings for us.

*Your servants in the Czech Republic.*  
(written by Mitravindā-devī dāsī)

## Dallas

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**“I was born in the darkest ignorance, and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him.”**

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

**“‘By the mercy of the guru, even a dumb man can become the greatest orator, and even a lame man can cross mountains.’ As advised by Lord Brahmā, one should remember this śāstric injunction if one desires success in his life.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **6.7.23purport)**

**Glorification of All your Disciples and Their Successions**

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept the most prostrated *sarvāṅga daṇḍavat praṇāmas* that our heart can command at the dust of your Divine Grace’s most magnanimous lotus feet.

All glories to Your Divine Grace! And all glories to ISKCON, your Hare Kṛṣṇa movement! These nondifferent from each other and yet full of variegatedness.

At the very outset, we wish to recognize a very stalwart general among the stalwart generals of your devotees, His Holiness Śrīla Tamāl Krishna Goswami Mahārāja. The glories of our very dear Goswami Mahārāja have been sung in many places at many times and in many circumstances, and they will continue to be sung everlastingly. And, Śrīla Prabhupāda, there are other great Vaiṣṇavas serving in so many countries all over the world, as stated in *Śrī Caitanya-bhāgavata:*

*ataeva sarva-deśe nija-bhakta-gaṇa*  
*avatīrṇa kaila śrī-caitanya-nārāyaṇa*

**“Therefore Śrī Caitanya, who is Nārāyaṇa Himself, had His devotees appear in all different countries.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as you know, our *dhāma* is called Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kālachandjī *dhāma,* which covers the whole area of northern Texas and the states of Oklahoma and Arkansas.

As stated in *Śrī Caitanya-bhāgavata,*

*yei deśe yāi kule vaiṣṇava ‘avatāre’*  
*tāṅhāra prabhāve lakṣa-yojana nistare*

**“In whatever place or family a Vaiṣṇava appears, people for thousands of miles around are delivered.”**

*ye-sthāne vaiṣṇava-gaṇa karena vijaya*  
*sei-sthāna haya ati-puṇya-tīrtha-maya*

**“Wherever the Vaiṣṇavas go, that place becomes a sanctified place of pilgrimage.”**

*saṁsāra tārite śrī-caitanya-avatāra*  
*āpane śrī-mukhe kariyāchena aṅgīkāra*

**“Śrī Caitanya descended to deliver the entire universe. He has confirmed this in His own words.”**

*śocya-deśe, śocya-kule āpana-samāna*  
*janmāiyā vaiṣṇave, sabāre kare trāṇa*

**“The Lord had His devotees appear at impious places and in impious families in order to deliver everyone.”**

We are now fully engaged in attempting to form a unified preaching plan (based on good organization, which itself is based on fine relationships based on love and trust) to spread your divine mission to the whole of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kālachandjī *dhāma,* because we know that in Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kālachandjī *dhāma* there are many, many more thousands of devotees (we now have a better understanding of the word “devotee”) than we know of.

When you first came to New York you explained to a person sitting next to you on a bench that you were clearly envisioning a grand ISKCON with many, many temples and devotees, and that only time was separating you from them. We hope and pray that our new plan will be as clearly envisioned.

We are at a critical juncture in the spreading of your most glorious mission, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Of course, nothing we do can be more crucial than what you did, and nothing can overshadow anything you did, because what you did was purely a miracle. Your activities were those of a very saintly and sincere preacher (some of us would say you were nothing less than a *śaktyāveśa-avatāra*), and we humble souls can never claim or even imagine to claim anything even approaching your stature, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we love you more dearly than our own lives and souls. The difficulty is that we are budding children, budding grandchildren and, dare we say, budding great-grandchildren, and as a result we sometimes make mistakes and do things that do not create love and trust, which you so much wanted to be the basis of your movement.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, one great improvement that has come about is that your movement has become more accepting of variegatedness in terms of strategies, while very faithfully maintaining intact your teachings in terms of philosophy. The philosophy you taught must remain unchanged for all time. As you said, the one credit you could accept is that you did not change you Spiritual master’s words (philosophy).In the same way, we must not change the philosophy, but in terms of the strategies to promote your mission, there can be many.

It is so nice to see that in your movement variegatedness in terms of strategies has now been accepted, at least to some degree. A great sign of this is that some of your disciples who had become so unhappy after your departure that they left your movement, or at least apparently left it, are now returning and being welcomed back with the widest possible arms because many are now recognizing that the very best assets you left behind, Śrīla Prabhupāda, were your disciples. Dare we say that? After all, many would argue that your books are the best assets you left us, and they would not be wrong. But the reason some say it is the disciples is because if we had read your books without their help, we would not have understood even a very small fraction of a very small fraction of what we have understood to date.

And, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we can also say that you are so great that you now have many grand-disciples, and some of these grand-disciples have become very strong *vartma-pradarśaka* gurus and *śikṣā-gurus,* and they are all preaching very nicely and very fervently and very faithfully within the boundaries of your mission.

In Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kālachandjī *dhāma,* Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have disciples of at least twenty-eight *dīkṣā-guru* disciples of yours, and many more disciples of *vartma-pradarśaka* and *śikṣā-guru* disciples of yours, and of your grand-disciples.

But again, Śrīla Prabhupāda, what can we say to praise you, and how do we express our deepest gratitude to you, because no amount of words in any amount of time with any number of mouths and tongues can glorify even a small fraction of your glories? Please give us some intelligence so we may learn to truly glorify you and your successions, to show some gratitude toward you and them, in terms of actions as well as words.

Thank you *ananta-koṭi* times, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are more dear to us than our own lives. Please bless us that we may always please you and never displease you.

All glories, all glories, all glories to Śrī Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga!  
All glories to you, our dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
All glories to your ISKCON, the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement!  
And all glories to your unrivaled disciplic successions!

*The servants of the servants of the servants of Your Divine Grace at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kālachandjī dhāma—North Texas, Oklahoma, and Arkansas, USA.*  
(written by Nityānanda dāsa Adhikārī)

## Denpasar

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances under your holy lotus feet.

Although we grand-disciples have known you mainly through your divine books, your divine voice, and your divine images (multimedia), we still feel very fortunate because you left with us your disciples and the living gurus, from whom we could hear, see, and feel Your Divine Grace’s nature, mission, and existence. Therefore those who are really sincere, who are honest and humble on this spiritual path—such souls will never forget the unpaid debt to Your Divine Grace.

So please kindly accept our deepest “Thank you!” Śrīla Prabhupāda.

The divine society you established has now spread quite far in Indonesia, and will become bigger and bigger and more and more complex and sophisticated. We strive for unity in diversity, something the materialists lack. But still, due to our immaturity, we feel the path to unity in the Indonesian *yātrā* is very long. As early as 1973 you wrote in a letter, “Our Gaudiya Math people fought with one another after the demise of Guru Maharaja, but my disciples have already begun fighting even in my presence.” So what will happen on us (your grand-disciples) in Indonesia, Śrīla Prabhupāda? I worry that we might not be able to resolve our differences and “agree to disagree,” as the politicians are fond of saying.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please kindly bestow your mercy on us grand-disciples, and on your direct disciples who are mainly in charge in Indonesia, so we may resolve our differences and push on this very great mission.

Hare Kṛṣṇa.

*Your servants at Sāndīpani Muni Āśrama, Denpasar, Bali, Indonesia.*  
(written by Agastya Muni dāsa)

## Denver

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*vāñchā-kalpa-tarubhyaś ca kṛpā-sindhubhya eva ca*  
*patitānāṁ pāvanebhyo vaiṣṇavebhyo namo namaḥ*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

My whole life changed when I met Your Divine Grace. After participating in the Vietnam War, I thought my consciousness had become like a rock and couldn’t be changed. After all the unusual circumstances surrounding that adventure, I did not know there was more excitement yet to come. I was totally against any kind of authority because of all the death I had seen it cause. After moving to Hollywood, I found a mantra card on top of a pay phone. It read: “To relieve all the anxieties of material life, please chant HARE KRISHNA...” I thought to myself, “Yes, this could be true,” and left it there for someone else to discover. After receiving a *Back to Godhead* magazine at Hollywood and Vine, going home and reading it, and agreeing it all made sense, I asked my girlfriend if she knew where the local Hare Kṛṣṇa temple was. She said, “Yes, I was born in the hospital across Venice Boulevard from the temple.” We went there and were immediately asked to dance around Tulasī-devī with the other devotees as the *pūjārī* purified everyone’s hand for watering Tulasī. We were told, “She’s a pure devotee.”

The second time we were at the temple we saw the woman who performed Tulasī-pūjā and said, “Look, there’s that pure devotee.” I had begun to read your *Bhagavad-gītā,* and on the second visit we were approached by a householder who asked us to stay with his family for three days to become accustomed to the practical living of the philosophy. He also mentioned that the you were coming. On the third day when I entered the temple room I found it almost empty. Everyone was at the airport picking you up. The ISKCON filmmaker, Yadubara dāsa, told me he was going to film the arrival address, and he needed me to hold a light over you on the *vyāsāsana* (I was about 6 foot 4). You entered in a very regal way, followed by at least five hundred devotees. I was surprised by your short statue, but by the way you held your head up, and from your being surrounded by the *sannyāsīs* with their *daṇḍas* and by the temple presidents, I could see you were the cynosure of all the devotees. You walked up to, climbed onto, and sat on the *vyāsāsana* so royally. You began to speak, and after ten minutes my arm felt like it was going to crack off my shoulder, but I knew this was important.

Without having seemed to notice me previously, halfway through your talk, with your head turned the other way, you paused and, while slowly turning your head to look directly into my eyes, said in a deep, grave voice, “Yesss.” It was as if you were saying “Yes, this even applies to you long-haired hippies.” I can’t even remember what philosophical point you were making! Your glance was like a laser beam entering my soul and exposing all my material contamination. Despite my completely arrogant stance against accepting any authority, I felt completely smashed. I knew right then that if there was one person in the world I could bow down to, it was you, and actually I had no choice. Surrender immediately became the most easy thing. This surrender, which I never felt possible in this life, left me with the feeling of having the same protection as a baby in its mother’s arms. Only by your mercy had I been transformed from a miserable drug-addicted Vietnam vet into a blissful shaven-headed devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa. Therefore, I will not be able to repay my debt to you in millions of lifetimes, nor praise you enough with millions of tongues. Your instruction to me was to “Just do something to help push on this movement. I am paying my obeisances to my spiritual master over and over, every day, and trying to follow his instructions. My only hope is to continue this relationship eternally!”

Your allowing me to remain in the association of devotees even though I am so fallen is proof of your potency. I am happily engaging in devotional service only by your mercy. I cannot imagine life without being around your blissful servants. With this whole Denver temple happily engaging in *guru-pūjā* every day, all the devotees can see how their suffering in the material world is being diminished. We wish to remain your humble servants.

Thank you very much.

*Your servants at the ISKCON temple in Denver, Colorado, USA.*  
(written by Rāmarājya dāsa)

## Devasadan Mandir

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

On this most glories day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, the whole world is celebrating your divine appearance 107 years ago. Being the savior of the whole world, you delivered the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu for the benefit of all humanity. It is only because you came to Detroit that we now we have a spiritual oasis—Devasadan Mandir—for the residents of Michigan.

Now all your devotees at Devasadan Mandir are preparing for the grand celebration of your 108thMahā-Vyāsa-pūjā next year. The most enthusiastic group comprises your dear grand-disciples, a group of youths who are working very hard to flood Detroit with Kṛṣṇa consciousness and to be ideal representatives of Your Divine Grace. They study your books, discuss them among themselves and their peers, and take responsibility for many programs at the temple. Under the leadership of Arjuna Prabhu, they have very high goals, so please guide them and bless them.

Your disciples and grand-disciples are working together in harmony to promote Kṛṣṇa consciousness by celebrating many festivals, such as Lord Jagannātha’s Ratha-yātrā, Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Janmāṣṭamī, and Govardhana pūjā. The new generation is being attracted and engaged by the Bhakti-Vṛkṣa program, which consists of two divisions, one under the leadership of Śrīmān Puruṣa Sūkta Prabhu, and the other under Śrīmān Āśiṣa Prabhu. Bhakti-Vṛkṣa regularly conducts home programs and university preaching programs. Under the leadership of Śrīmān Mādhava dāsa Prabhu and Śrīmān Parāśara Prabhu’s families, the Sunday feast program is flourishing very well. Our Sunday school program is being nourished by Śrīmān Śrīnivāsan Prabhu and his family. The Garden Committee, under Śrīmān Caitanya Rūpa Prabhu and Śrīmān Pāṇḍu Prabhu, is planning to bring back the glories of the original gardens. Her Grace Jāmbavatī-devī dāsī, with the help of a team of devotees, is working very hard to bring many souls to enjoy worshiping Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjabihārī at our “Featured Friday” program and interfaith seminars. Your loving disciple Her Grace Cintāmaṇi-devī dāsī is employing her skills by organizing the most attractive cooking classes. And the crown jewel of Devasadan Mandir, our Deity worship team, under Śrīmān Caitanya Rūpa Prabhu and Śrīmān Girigovardhana Prabhu, is always increasing the quality of services to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjabihārī.

Finally, dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, we would like to thank you with all our hearts for giving us the most loving, caring, and dynamic GBC leaders, namely His Grace Ravīndra Svarūpa Prabhu, His Holiness Bhaktitīrtha Mahārāja, and Her Grace Mālatī Prabhu. Please hold our hands very tight so that we will always remain with you in the service of Kṛṣṇa until we leave our bodies.

*Your humble servants at Devasadan Mandir, in Detroit, Michigan, USA.*  
(written by Jagad-Guru dāsa)

## East Hartford

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

At present the U.S. is at war with Iraq. You once said, “When there is real, righteous fight for good cause, that fighting is all right....So these wars are declared not for Kṛṣṇa’s sake; by the politicians’ whims.”

Actually, there *is* a righteous war going on right now. It is the war your followers have declared on Māyā. When I first joined your International Society for Krishna Consciousness, I was told that joining this movement was declaring war on Māyā, and that when you declare war on Māyā, she will fight back. Knowing that bit of information helped me prepare for the fight. When Māyā presented her attractions, or when she made Kṛṣṇa consciousness seem impossible, I knew it was just another one of her tricks, and I was mentally prepared for it. Now, over thirty years later, the war is still going on. If I stick with the ground I have achieved, Māyā seems to pretty much leave me alone. But if and when I try to take more ground and advance further, she doesn’t let me have it without a fight.

In Chapter Eight of *The Path of Perfection,* you state:

To pursue the transcendental path is more or less to declare war on the illusory energy. When we accept any process of self-realization, we are actually declaring war against *māyā,* illusion, and *māyā* is certain to place many difficulties before us. Therefore, there is a chance of failure, but one has to become very steady. Whenever a person tries to escape the clutches of the illusory energy, she tries to defeat the practitioner by various allurements. A conditioned soul is already allured by the modes of material energy, and there is every chance of being allured again, even while performing transcendental disciplines.

I pray to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for the strength to continue this fight against *māyā* and not become allured by her illusions. Every time I get more serious about Kṛṣṇa consciousness, *māyā* makes my life more difficult. It seems to me that she has some spies over in my camp, and as soon as I try to take the next hill, she is ready and waiting, anticipating my attack.

In an early lecture on the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* you said,

Rūpa Gosvāmī and Sanātana Gosvāmī were appointed as the commander in chief...in this fighting with Māyā....If anyone wants to be expert in Kṛṣṇa consciousness to fight with Māyā, they must follow the principles of Rūpa Gosvāmī and Sanātana Gosvāmī.

I am weary and need help. I am humbly requesting you, and also our commanders Rūpa and Sanātana Gosvāmīs, to please send reinforcements in the form of advanced devotee association. I am hoping this war will be over soon, but it can go on for hundreds and even thousands of years, spanning many lifetimes. I don’t know how many previous lives I have been fighting this war, but I do know I have been engaged for over thirty years in this life. I am praying at your lotus feet and the lotus feet of our commanders that I don’t have to come back again to finish this fight with Māyā.

Thank you for helping us prepare for battle and arming us with the only weapons that can defeat Māyā: *śravaṇaṁ kīrtanaṁ viṣṇoḥ smaraṇaṁ pāda-sevanam/ arcanaṁ vandanaṁ dāsyaṁ sakhyam ātma-nivedanam.* Please help all of us to learn how to use these weapons properly so that we can defeat Māyā, cross over the ocean of birth and death, and go back home, back to Godhead. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for all you have given us.

Reporting from the battlefield, and hopefully getting closer to seeing you and Lord Kṛṣṇa on the other side of the war zone,

*Your humble servant soldiers, wounded but still fighting, at the ISKCON temple in East Hartford, Connecticut, USA.*  
(written by Pyāri Mohan dāsa)

## Eger

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances! All glories to you, who save the fallen souls! Praying to your lotus feet, we ask you to please accept this humble offering from the devotees of Eger, Hungary, on the occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā.

By trying to serve you according to your *vāṇī,* which you consider more important than your *vapu,* we try to please you. Actually, the process is simple: we just should follow your instructions and try to fulfill your desires, because your desires are Kṛṣṇa’s desires as well. You couldn’t forget Kṛṣṇa, even for one moment—this is one quality of a pure devotee. We have read in your books that a pure devotee never forgets Kṛṣṇa and that Kṛṣṇa reciprocates by never forgetting His pure devotee. Once you said that when you are preaching Kṛṣṇa is standing next to you and approving what you say. We can’t measure your greatness, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Devotees all around the world are trying to serve you, from morning until evening, to express their appreciation for what you have done for the world and for us. Here in Eger we have a Food for Life program (we distribute *prasādam* every Saturday), book distribution is going on nicely, public preaching programs are frequent, and PR preaching is very recognized in our country.

We trust you are happy to learn that this year’s Padayātrā, the third annual one, will see more than 35 devotees doing *harināma* and other forms of preaching for three months during the summer, across more than 150 communities and 900 kilometers. We are preparing ourselves for this program very carefully, and we are praying to you that this mission will be successful.

We need you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda. We don’t want to live without you, we don’t want to forget you. We want to be with you and help you as a loving son always helps his father. Our lives have become very pleasant after we met you. We must really be souls, not the body, because from a bodily relationship there is no feeling like this. We are very immature, and all our activities are covered by mistakes and selfish motivations. But now we throw all these aside and, with lifted arms, exclaim, “*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda, all glories, all victory to you!”

*Your devotees in Eger, Hungary.*  
(written by Tīrtha-pāda dāsa)

## Ekaterinburg

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances unto the dust of your lotus feet. All glories unto you!

We are so thankful to you for your infinite compassion upon the conditioned souls. Your love and care for us are so great, and your heart is so loving, that any fallen soul may take your shelter. And this is wonderful. Your activities are so nice that they attract many souls. Your desire to give Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the people is so strong that your movement continues to develop, even in your physical absence, although one may feel your presencethrough your devoted disciples. Your books are wise, and the holy names of the Lord you taught us are full of sweetness. The process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness is so powerful that it can change any hard heart.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, ants cannot understand how the planes fly, and before wecame in contact with you through your movement we were like those ants. But by your causeless mercy even the dumbcan speak and the blind can see. You havethe strong desire to help us return back to Godhead, and you are giving us the spiritual strength to change our consciousness. We are made of your mercy. Please continue to lead us, so that we may become pure devotees of the Lord. May your instructions always live in our hearts so that we will continue assisting you in spreading the holy names of the Lord all over the world.

We feel your mercy and the mercy of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu here in Ekaterinburg. The devotees have many Deities of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi at their homes, where conditioned souls may meet and chant the holy names of the Lord, worship the Deities, and discuss the philosophy of the *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. There are nice Hare Kṛṣṇa festivals in Russia, in India, and all over the world. Everyone may come and take part and be happy. The process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness is so simple and ecstatic that anyone may join in!

Thank you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, for everything you are doing for us! Please pour your mercy on us always!

*Harināma-saṅkīrtana kī jaya*!

*Your servants and followers in Ekaterinburg, Russia.*

## Ghana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Once again the time has come for devotees the world over to offer their heartfelt prayers and appreciations to you as reciprocation for your boundless mercy, which has transformed their lives.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your great sacrifice you have saved the world from gliding down to hell.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your great determination you endured a lot of difficulties just to distribute Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mercy throughout the world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your great compassion you have delivered *mlecchas,* Yavanas, and Rākṣasas from the path *saṁsāra*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your unflinching faith in the words of your Guru Mahārāja you published many transcendental literatures for the benefit of the entire human race.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your great love for Kṛṣṇa you have given love of Godhead to the world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your great purity you have made many pure.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your deep knowledge of Kṛṣṇa you have enriched the world with spiritual knowledge.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your exemplary life many have surrendered their lives to you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your strong preaching, devotional service to Kṛṣṇa has spread throughout the world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your great potency you have brought the spiritual world closer to all sincere souls.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your inconceivable mercy crows have become swans fully absorbed in singing the glories of the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa.

On this wonderful occasion of your divine appearance, I am praying that you kindly free this one crow of all bad qualities so that I may be a better servant of your devotees.

*Your servants in Ghana.*  
(written by Śrīvāsa dāsa)

## Gītā-nāgarī

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

On this most auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā anniversary, we at Gītā-nāgarī offer our heartfelt thanks to you. “Thank you” doesn’t seem sufficient, yet it is from our hearts. Thank you for showing us that we can leave our home for our spiritual master’s mission, just as you left Vṛndāvana. Thank you for showing us that we can come through great hardships when we are fixed on Kṛṣṇa and Guru, just as you traveled on that boat to America. Thank you for showing us that even though the body is old and deteriorating, if we remain fixed on serving Guru and Kṛṣṇa, we can continue serving. Thank you for showing us how to leave this body at death and, with our very last breath, still remember Kṛṣṇa. Thank you for showing us the determination and strong desire to follow Lord Caitanya’s and your guru’s instructions, which took you to every town and village, including Gītā-nāgarī, in Port Royal, Pennsylvania.

Gītā-nāgarī is a very special place because of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Knowing that this is where you were coming to complete your mission in 1977 makes it more special, and we are very fortunate to be here. What can we offer you, who gave us so much love and guidance to push us along the path of *bhakti* so that we could get to know Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa? We are endeavoring to honor you by working toward the fulfillment of your desire for a self-sufficient farm community here.

We realize that Gītā-nāgarī is going to be what each of us wants to see happen and what each of us does to make it happen. As they year 2003 began, we were inspired to think about what we want for Gītā-nāgarī for the next three years. These are some of the desires. Some are completed and others are in progress.

With Their Lordships settled in Their new temple for the last two years, we plan to beautify the grounds around the temple with flowerbeds, benches, bird feeders, and fountains. In this beautiful environment, we plan to build a guesthouse to offer seekers of truth comfortable shelter. Between our safe, contented cows munching grass in the pastures and the peacocks strutting as they show their beautiful feathers, we will all get a glimpse of Vṛndāvana.

Our children’s practices and lifestyle are nourishing their Kṛṣṇa consciousness and their ability to succeed in any environment. They are also being trained as a *bhajan* group. They play harmonium, *mṛdaṅgas* and *karatālas* very nicely, and their voices ring out so sweetly as they chant the Holy Name.

A Tulasī house is complete. There are about ten plants doing very nicely. They can be seen and offered obeisances from both outside and inside the temple. It is very pleasing to the mind to sit with Tulasī-devī in her room and chant.

We have a crew of gardeners who are enthusiastic and committed to planting and caring for flowers and vegetables year round. In this way we can continually offer Their Lordships flowers from Gītā-nāgarī land and fresh vegetables for their meals. Because of your mercy and your desire to see Gītā-nāgarī develop as a self-sufficient community, we are motivated to do that. There is an established soap business here, and the community store is open. We hope to develop a cooperative market soon.

During the week our devotees go on *saṅkīrtana* to State College, where we have a preaching program that is growing. Many of the students and their guests come to the Sunday program at the farm, which is a wonderful preaching opportunity.

We are all very excited about our commitment to make Gītā-nāgarī a place that you will be proud of. We beg for your mercy and your blessings as we endeavor to increase and improve our *sādhana,* purify our hearts, completely surrender to Their Lordships, live by the standards you gave us, and become fully Kṛṣṇa conscious.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we want to please you and show you that your desires are being fulfilled. We want to follow your instructions. Please bless us with the determination to continue until Gītā-nāgarī is what you desire.

At present there are only a few of us here in Gītā-nāgarī, but our strength lies in our desire to serve you and please you under the guidance of your son and loving servant, Bhakti Tīrtha Swami. We are doing this not just by physical endeavors but also by establishing loving relationships and serving together as a team.

We will never forget that we are indebted to you.

*Your servants at the Gītā-nāgarī farm community, Port Royal, Pennsylvania, USA.*

## Gītā-nāgarī Baru

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet! You are the only shelter of all surrendered souls.

Last year I sent you a small report on our activities at Gītā-nāgarī Baru (New Gītā-nāgarī). On behalf of the devotees living there, I would like to give you an update of our present activities.

You will be pleased to know that we now have twenty-two families living in our small Vaiṣṇava village, bringing the total number of residents to sixty-two, of whom thirty are children. Our devotees remain mostly involved in working the land and trying to live a simple Kṛṣṇa conscious life, as you have so nicely directed us to do. We also have six of our devotees teaching in close-by schools, and some of them will soon start our own Vaiṣṇava school at Gītā-nāgarī Baru.

The whole atmosphere is becoming more and more pleasing as we see devotees going about their business in devotee attire chanting the holy name all day long. We recently celebrated the Gaura-Pūrṇimā festival. It was attended by some sixty guests, bringing the number of devotees at the festival to over 120. On that very day we organized two *parikramās*. One carried Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi around the entire boundary of our land, a total of four kilometers. Some of our guests were from India and Malaysia, and for them it was an unforgettable experience. Our second *parikramā* carried Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-Gopāla, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and you in your *mūrti* form within the residential area of our land. All were taken on our bullock cart pulled by your servant, our bull, Param Dharma Prabhu. The cart stopped in front of every home, and the families offered incense, lamp, and flowers to the Lord. In addition, each home offered some cooked *bhoga* along with some items grown from their land. By the end of our visit to the twenty-two homes around the residential area, devotees had to carry the various vegetables offered from their gardens in a wheel barrel: there were so many varieties of items grown—a wonderful sight to behold!

Some time in December last year we were fortunate to be visited by your servant Tejiyas Prabhu, who spent a few days giving discourses on crop rotation and compost. This very much enlivened the devotees, who noted down his many good suggestions. Our devotees, although mostly from farming backgrounds, still have many things to learn about farming the more natural and Vedic way.

By the time you will read this offering, our Śrī Viṣṇu Sahasra Nāma Vana project will have been officially inaugurated. This project was inspired by one of your Life Members and close supporters from Jakarta, Mr. Siva, and executed in large part by your servant His Grace Gaura-Maṇḍala-Bhūmi Prabhu. It is a unique project involving practically all the residents of Gītā-nāgarī Baru. I have personally trekked most of the four-kilometer path around our land, where the one thousand fruit trees have been planted. Some are bearing fruit even at this early stage. Due to our small rivers on the north side of the land, we have had to make a few ghats and bridges. In one place the two ghats help join a span of water close to 40 meters long and 1.5 meters deep. Our resident devotee Jatāyu Prabhu has built a small boat that we have named *Jaladuta* in memory of your first trip to America. And to help us remember your place of worship, Śrī Navadvīpa-Māyāpur *dhāma,* we have named the two ghats “Ghat Māyāpur” and “Ghat Navadvīpa. The first to travel on the *Jaladuta* will be Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-Gopāla, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

We now have only two cows and one bull—Lakṣmī, Nārāyaṇī, and Param Dharma, respectively. We’re sorry to report that we have lost our small bull Ekādaśī within three months of his being born. We also lost another calf at the moment of delivery, and subsequently his mother also passed away. We called the veterinarian in each case, but he could not help them. He also could not clearly diagnose the cause of their premature departure. Maintaining cows and bulls, we are coming to understand, is not so easy. Please forgive us for any neglect on our part, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Our devotees are learning to be more cautious in looking after Lord Kṛṣṇa’s cows.

We are now searching for additional land so as to accommodate more devotees who would like to live at Gītā-nāgarī Baru. Actually, we need to create similar small villages around Indonesia, and we pray that you will kindly bless us so we will become successful in our small attempt at pleasing you. For if we fail to please the Lord’s pure devotee, surely Kṛṣṇa will also not be pleased.

On behalf of all the devotees at Gītā-nāgarī Baru, thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Our devotees are sincerely thankful to you for giving them such an opportunity to live among devotees in a peaceful and Kṛṣṇa conscious atmosphere. Please continue to shower your blessings upon this humble attempt to establish rural *varṇāśrama* life.

*Your servants at Gītā-nāgarī Baru village, a farming community in Lampung, Sumatra, Indonesia.*  
(written by Bhakti Rāghava Swami)

## Gṭthenburg

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my obeisances to His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at the lotus feet of the transcendental Lord.**

**“I offer my obeisances unto you O spiritual master, servant of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī. You are so kindly teaching the message of Lord Caitanya and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with voidism and impersonalism.”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

You have taught us that the Supreme Absolute is a person, that the infinitesimal part-and-parcel *jīva* soul is also eternally a person, and that true love consists of personal dealings between the two. Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s teachings, as presented by Your Divine Grace, dispel the darkness of voidism and impersonalism.

It takes a person to save another. Any fatal catastrophe in this dangerous world will prove this fact. The task of saving life is such a delicate thing that its performance has to be undertaken ultimately by a person. Similarly, saving the soul from all kinds of material and spiritual bewilderment is an unavoidably personal act. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are that person who considered the order of your spiritual master to be your heart and soul. You are the one who selflessly took on the work of trying to save us all.

Our prayer to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is that we may be able to respond fully to what you’ve offered. As it takes a person to save another, of course that also goes for the one being saved. By remaining unconscious, unwilling, and mechanical, one simply remains a hopeless case. Let us take to the process of hearing and chanting with rapt attention. Let us build on the determination to offer our hearts. Let us understand in a personal way what will please you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Because pleasing you means pleasing Lord Kṛṣṇa, which in reality is indeed our life and soul.

*Your servants at Govinda’s Restaurant and Hare Kṛṣṇa Temple in Gṭthenburg, Sweden.*  
(written by Bhakta Ricard Tsterstam)

## Guyana

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

On this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā celebration, we would like to express our eternal gratitude to you for changing our situation from a morbid, sordid, and hopeless state to being gentle servants within your house, the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. Today we meditate on and try to appreciate your appearance in this world and the unique benediction you gave us—an opportunity to be part of your mission to save all humanity. You epitomize the full meaning of phrase *para-duḥkha-duḥkhī*.

Your entire life is most glorious. But especially during the last twelve years of your manifest *līlā* on this planet, you, like the Supreme Personality of Godhead, performed so many amazing feats and transcendentally sweet superhuman pastimes. You appeared in order to fulfill the desires of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and the previous *ācāryas*. Your transcendental activities are so pure and relishable that meditating on your life can keep us firmly anchored to the path of devotional service.

We want to especially thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your causeless mercy in the shape of your books, your dedicated and loyal followers, the wonderful Kṛṣṇa conscious festivals, your temples, *prasādam,* and everything else that you left us. For these our debt to you is unlimited and shall remain so eternally.

Recently, while browsing over the VedaBase, we were amazed to find that as far back as 1969 you received an invitation to visit Guyana, and later you were considering visiting here. For one reason or another, that visit did not manifest. But you continued to discuss Guyana, and in 1971 you expressed the desire to open an ISKCON Center in Georgetown, Guyana. You mentioned that this would be a great boon to the Guyanese society.

While you were still on the planet, you sent several of your faithful disciples to preach the message of Lord Caitanya here, and you monitored their successes closely. For several years just after you closed your earthly pastimes, dedicated local devotees assisted by your senior disciples, who visit from time to time, took your desires to heart. From 1977 to 1997, preaching on your behalf was done from two centers—one in Berbice and the other in Demerara.

By your blessings, over the last five years the Guyana *yātrā* was able to expand, completing three new temples. The first, New Kulīnagrāma, finished in 1999, is home for Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulacandra. In the year 2000 the Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi Ashirvaad Mandir was opened in West Demerara, and on Janmāṣṭamī last year the ornate Essequibo Mandir was opened. We are confident that if we simply remain faithful and steadfast in our resolve to please you, you will be kind and give us the intelligence to preach with success.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, Guyana was not fortunate enough to be blessed with the dust of your lotus feet, and we were never able to see you. But it is described that it is more important to hear from the guru than to see him. Please bless us so that we will forever hear and follow your instructions, which you have delineated so loud and clear.

Kindly let us always remain simple-hearted and happy with all you have given us. In this remote part of the world, we feel secure and sheltered within your ISKCON, blessed by so many wonderful Vaiṣṇavas you sent and continue to send.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for saving us and giving us this beautiful path of *bhakti*.

*Your servants from the four ISKCON Temples in Guyana, South America.*  
(written by Paramātmā dāsa)

## Hare Krishna Dhām

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your infinite love, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Every morning we thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. At a very advanced age, when most people look for an easy chair to live the life of retirement, you decide to go to a dangerous, ignorant foreign land and give *bhakti-yoga* to anyone who might listen.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your aspiring servants in Hare Krishna Dhām, Houston, Texas, USA.*

## Helsinki

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

It is amazing how powerfully your books are changing the world. In Finland about one million books have been distributed, and many results are already there. The terms of Kṛṣṇa conscious philosophy are spoken everywhere. ISKCON is legally well established, and we just got very nice rights to teach our own children in the schools among the other official religions in Finland. There is also an effort to get some economic support from the Finnish government.

So many nice signs are there that this wonderful message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness is being preached more and more here in Finland. For over ten years there has been a traveling *saṅkīrtana* party doing programs in fourteen cities of this country, and we are doing college programs in more than one hundred colleges around the country.

So for sure the glories of Kṛṣṇa and His wonderful servants, especially Your Divine Grace, are being spread more and more.

This has all been possible because of Your Divine Grace’s wonderful disciples who come here and give us their blessings. Every year about ten *sannyāsīs* come here to Finland and give us a direct link to Your Divine Grace’s mercy. We beg at your lotus feet that we will continue to receive such blessings in the future.

Although the preaching field is becoming broader and there is so much more to do, please don’t let us lose track of the happy and active practice of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We don’t want to become just one of those official religions without the spirit.

Please let us continue with these simple activities and develop the quality of our practice at the same time.

*Your servants at the Helsinki temple and throughout Finland.*  
(written by Tattvavāda dāsa)

## Kiev

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

*śrī-guru-caraṇa-padma, kevala-bhakati-sadma,*  
*bando mui sāvadhāna mate*  
*jāhāra prasāde bhāi, e bhava toriyā jāi,*  
*kṛṣṇa-prāpti hoy jāhā ha’te*

**“The lotus feet of our spiritual master are the only way by which we can attain pure devotional service. I bow to his lotus feet with great awe and reverence. By his grace one can cross the ocean of material suffering and obtain the mercy of Kṛṣṇa.”**

On this auspicious anniversary of your coming to this material world, the minds of thousands of your disciples and followers are concentrated upon you. On this day meditation upon you infinitely deepens, because it is you who brought about a revolution in the lives of all mankind, who, before long, will begin living according to your books for the next ten thousand years. Great scientists, philosophers, and politicians speak about peace and welfare so much—they write voluminous books—but nevertheless all of them fail to realize their dreams and really become happy. Why? You explain:

Therefore, if you want peace at all, you will have to change your consciousness into Kṛṣṇa consciousness, both individually and collectively, by the simple process of chanting the holy name of God. This is a standard and recognized process for achieving peace in the world. We therefore recommend that everyone become Kṛṣṇa conscious by chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare. \[*The Science of Self-Realization,* “The Peace Formula”\]

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you did something that nobody else could do. You came here to this material world to give people a chance at freedom and happiness—people who had completely forgotten the Lord, being absorbed in material activity. Your compassion upon the conditioned souls knows no bounds. There can be no duty or obligation for us but to serve you.

*evaṁ janaṁ nipatitaṁ prabhavāhi-kūpe*  
*kāmābhikāmam anu yaḥ prapatan prasaṅgāt*  
*kṛtvātmasāt surarṣiṇā bhagavan gṛhītaḥ*  
*so ‘haṁ kathaṁ nu visṛje tava bhṛtya-sevām*

**“My dear Lord, O Supreme Personality of Godhead, because of my association with material desires, one after another, I was gradually falling into a blind well full of snakes, following the general populace. But Your servant Nārada Muni kindly accepted me as his disciple and instructed me how to achieve this transcendental position. Therefore, my first duty is to serve him. How could I leave his service?” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **7.9.28)**

A disciple can never pay back the debt to his spiritual master. The only thing he can do is accept the instructions emanating from the lotus lips of his guru as the goal of his life and dedicate himself to following these instructions. This is the essence of a disciple’s life.

*guru-mukha-padma-vākya, cittete koriyā aikya,*  
*ār nā koriho mane āśā*  
*śrī-guru-caraṇe rati, ei se uttama gati,*  
*je prasāde pure sarva āśā*

**“My only wish is to have my consciousness purified by the words emanating from his lotus mouth. Attachment to his lotus feet is the perfection that fulfils all desires.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray that you are satisfied with our insignificant service. Allow us to continue this service to your mission, which is to promote the *saṅkīrtana* movement all over the world.

Construction of a magnificent temple—New Navadvīpa Mandir—is going on here in Kiev. The temple will become the Center of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Ukraine and show the glory of Lord Kṛṣṇa and His pure devotees like you. Many people are becoming familiar with your movement and becoming your followers. All this must be pleasing to you.

*śrī-guru karuṇā-sindhu, adhama janāra bandbu,*  
*lokanāth lokera jīvana*  
*hā hā prabhu koro doyā, deho more pada-chāyā,*  
*ebe jaśa ghuṣuk tribhuvana*

**“Our spiritual master is the ocean of mercy, the friend of the poor, and the lord and master of the devotees. O master! Be merciful unto me. Give me the shade of your lotus feet. Your fame is spread all over the three worlds.”**

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for everything that you have done! All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your servants in Kiev, Ukraine.*

## Lalitā Pura

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, how merciful you were to come to this material world to bring some hope to us fallen souls! When the day for your departure finally arrived, you gave this last instruction to Acyutānanda Prabhu: “Just pray to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa that I can go to America.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you taught us that if we preach we will become strong in Kṛṣṇa consciousness and we will understand all the teachings of the *śāstra* in the proper perspective. But if we stop our preaching, you taught, then everything will become stagnated, and we will lose our enthusiasm. This we have seen to be true in our lives.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are so kind—wherever you go or stay you preach the glories of the Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. We would like to totally surrender unto your lotus feet, to forget the demands of our stupid minds and only blindly serve you and Kṛṣṇa. Please give us the intelligence to be come more serious.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you live forever by your divine instructions, and your sincere followers live with you. Please let us become your sincere followers.

*Your aspiring servants in Lalitā-pura Seva-kuñja Āśrama, Tangkiling, Palangkaraya, Central Borneo, Indonesia.*

## Las Vegas

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

On this day of your Vyāsa-pūjā I would like to thank you very, very much for the inconceivable mercy you have bestowed upon me by letting me continue this most sublime process of self-realization, Kṛṣṇa consciousness, in your ISKCON movement.

I am one among thousands and thousands of very grateful people who admire your loving sacrifice for humanity, your inconceivable effort to point society in the direction of real civilization. The perfect example of your own life, your teachings, and all your arrangements for establishing and developing your ISKCON preaching movement are unique in the history of mankind.

Even God Himself didn’t perform this task of spreading His own glories all over the earth. Rather, He entrusted this incredible task to you. And you succeeded on His behalf in such a short time! Christianity and Islam needed so much time to be recognized and known all over the world, but because you were empowered by the Lord, you needed only twelve years to widely spread the glories of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. And your presented Śrī Kṛṣṇa *as He is*—that is, as He is described in the ancient Vedic scriptures and in accordance with the teachings of the previous *ācāryas* coming down in the line of disciplic succession.

Two things that would ordinarily be done separately—establishing a civilized standard of life for human beings and establishing the supremacy of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa—you have miraculously done simultaneously. Nobody before in human history on this planet accomplished such an incredible task of spreading the authentic words of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, as you did.

Not only did you reacquaint the world with the civilized standard of life, but you simultaneously miraculously introduced the most materialistic and degraded people of Western world to devotional service to the Supreme Lord.

It is inconceivable that after age of seventy, when ordinary people are usually inactive, you were extremely active. At such an age, ordinary people usually think about death, but you were full of life, and you were giving life to others by your enlivening preaching and activities. Simultaneously, you were always thinking about how to bring spiritual life back to the consciousness of human society.

In this way, as the greatest sage of the modern age, as well as its most intimate devotee of the Supreme Lord and foremost well-wisher of all living beings, you brought to life all the essential Vedic scriptures. And because of your pure love of Godhead you were empowered to implement this transcendental knowledge among the modern degraded people in general. Because you were always thinking about how to spread the glories of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa and establish new centers of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in places where He was unknown, all your sincere followers were moved to follow in your footsteps. Therefore, for your pleasure, and by your mercy and the blessings of your sincere followers, I am now in Las Vegas trying to spread the glories of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa in our newly-established ISKCON center.

At present, Las Vegas is the most dynamically developing city in the world. Every month around 7,000 people move here, and they need God consciousness, Kṛṣṇa consciousness, in their lives. Only by your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, will it be possible to inject Kṛṣṇa consciousness into the hearts of these people.

And only by your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, was it possible for us to remain in Las Vegas for more than one year and establish an ISKCON Center. Officially, we are holding regular Sunday programs and worshiping the Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi Deity. Also by your mercy, we are performing regular public programs in one of the biggest local libraries, and we have established the ISKCON Club at the university, conducting regular programs for students there. Your books are also distributed there. Our center is visited by many ISKCON swamis, as well as your other strict disciples, who are encouraging us to develop greater dedication in service to your lotus feet.

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept my humble entreaty for more mercy in the form of spiritual strength, so we can continue with enthusiasm and determination. I beg you to please help us contribute to your mission by spreading and firmly establishing the glories of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa in this world-famous yet extremely sinful city—a city that is, for a devotee, nondifferent from hell.

I am begging you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to please let me remain at your lotus feet in devotional service, for your satisfaction. Please help my mind be completely focused on your desires, my intelligence completely absorbed in your instructions, my senses completely engaged in Kṛṣṇa conscious activities, and my behavior completely in line with Kṛṣṇa conscious culture. With your help I want to open my heart widely so that you can fill it with pure love of Kṛṣṇa.

My life belongs to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and my future is in your hands. I am depending on your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your servants in Los Vegas, Nevada, USA.*  
(written by Surapāla dāsa)

## Lisbon

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances.

You have mentioned many times that Kṛṣṇa consciousness is a science. If the process is correctly followed, we will obtain positive and visible results. If not, there will be significant complications. Therefore, we devotees must pay attention and be more careful in the practice of this science, so that one day the world will recognize us as the most distinguished servants among all God’s servants, who are able guide humanity from the depths of depravity to the highest liberation.

So, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we must be consonant with your wishes, with your priorities, and with your understandings, so that we can appreciate the gifts you have left us as our inheritance, beginning with the treasury of spiritual knowledge to which we now have access. If we do so, one day—maybe sooner than we can imagine—we will be qualified to be your real followers.

Cooperation and sharing of insights must exist before spiritual competition, as genuine as that might be. For then each devotee will give the best of himself to your mission and share his skills and realizations with all. If this does not happen, the institution you so painstakingly built with your unbroken faith in Kṛṣṇa will be transformed into a center of politics, divisiveness, and criticism, and the relationships between its members will be based in a lack of respect for one another and be devoid of genuine Vaiṣṇava etiquette.

We must implement, as soon as possible, systematic training of devotees, with the aim of making our movement stable and peaceful—a state that will not be attained automatically. If we fail to do this, we may become little more than a masquerade party of so-called *brahmacārīs, gṛhasthas,* and *sannyāsīs*, when our real party is the chanting party glorifying the holy names of God. First we must verify if the devotees are correctly placed in their *āśrama,* as well as in the services they are assigned. This is essential for preaching as well as a peaceful and happy ISKCON society. If we fail to do this, we will lose precious opportunities for expanding Kṛṣṇa consciousness. As you taught us, we must be engaged according to our psychophysical nature and at the same time preach Kṛṣṇa’s consciousness. This is the only way to give credibility to our service to you.

It is not necessary to be a big expert on the *śāstras*. It is enough to follow the process with honesty and trust sincerely in you. We must read your thoughts again and again, and from them we must imbibe the precious lessons that are most necessary for us, such as to be determined and patient.

Once a devote asked you (and I paraphrase):

Now you are among us and everything is all right, but as time goes by, when you will be here no more and our society may fall from the standard, what shall happen?

You answered (paraphrase):

You are all important members of our society, so you must work to correct it, but *do not leave it*.

Keeping steady in our service to you is, no doubt, a big challenge in our lives, but it is essential to do so, because only by your satisfaction can we be free from birth and death. We hope that in our next life we can serve you in a more adequate way.

*Your humble servants in Lisbon, Portugal.*

## Lome

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Kindly accept our respectful prostrated obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to the *saṅkīrtana* mission of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu! All glories , all glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for fulfilling the mission of Lord Caitanya!

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

**Kṛṣṇa consciousness is spreading to every town and village, as predicted by Lord Caitanya, only because you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, acted as a perfect instrument for the Lord to carry out His mission. There were certainly many learned** ***ācāryas*** **in the Vaiṣṇava line, but you were the one chosen to deliver the mercy of** ***kṛṣṇa-bhakti*** **to the rest of the world.**

At the time Your Divine Grace left India with the sole aim of executing the order of your Guru Mahārāja—to print books and preach in the Western world—there were many Vedāntists, Śrīpāds, and *mahā-bhāgavatas* in India, but you were the one whom the Lord was pleased with.

Today the many people around the world who are being attracted to Kṛṣṇa consciousness testify to your greatness. By the power of your pure preaching and empowered commentaries on the Vedic teachings, more and more centers are being opened around the world. This year I am presenting the offering of the devotees in Togo, a Francophone country in West Africa, where we opened a new ISKCON center here in Lome in February of this year.

Many of the devotees here have been in contact with Lord Caitanya’s mission since in the mid-80’s. Some have been ordering your books in French from Canada and Paris and have been practicing Kṛṣṇa consciousness at home simply by reading your books. Another group of about twenty young men have also been holding *nāma-haṭṭa* meetings, trying to study your books without having ever had any contact with a temple devotee.

Visiting the homes of these devotees, one would naturally think he has been transported into the homes of people with long familiarity with the Vedic tradition.

I was filled with pride in being your grand-disciple when I met a Togolese gentleman, the National Head of Hygiene and Sanitation, who has compiled his prayers and system of worship through the study of your books. His absorption in the Vedic literature is beyond what I can describe here.

But there are others who have similarly being practicing for many years while praying fervently for the day a temple of Lord Kṛṣṇa would be opened in their country. When I saw the devotion to, faith in, and love for Kṛṣṇa in people who for the most part have never met a devotee before, but who have simply come in contact with your books, my faith intensified.

I came to Lome alone in October 2002, and after five months we had a full-fledged temple, a family of devotees, and an active congregation. All this is only a testimony to your greatness, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

My prayer to you on this auspicious anniversary of your appearance is that you will bless me, and the devotees with me, with your potency so that we can continue spreading the mission of Lord Caitanya to every country in West Africa. There are many countries in this part of the world where the people are literally starving for want of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

I pray that every year from now on, when I write your Vyāsa-pūjā homage, I can inform you about our new conquests, about many new centers opened and many devotees made. In this way Lord Caitanya’s prediction, which you worked so hard to fulfill, will continue to be fulfilled.

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

**The holy name of the Lord will be chanted in every household and in every town and village.**

Bless us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so that we can continue to receive your mercy through your able representatives, our gurus and GBCs, His Holiness Bhakti Tīrtha Swami and His Holiness Kavicandra Swami. I pray that we can be perfect instruments for our gurus mission, just as you were for your guru.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

*Your servants in Lome, Togo.*  
(written by Varāha dāsa)

## London (Soho)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

In the year 2002, the devotees of the Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa temple in central London continued to spread your movement with great enthusiasm and determination.

Our *saṅkīrtana* devotees, headed by Rādhā Raman Prabhu, Khela-tīrtha Prabhu, and Bhakta James Edwards distributed more than 87,000 of your books (more than three times the previous year’s distribution). Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, your books are the torchlights of knowledge meant for opening the eyes of those born in the darkest ignorance. You mercifully pushed us to mercifully distribute them to all materially conditioned human beings. We offer our respectful obeisances unto you and beg for your mercy so that we may maintain and increase our distribution of your books.

From our temple a *harināma* party goes out daily (sometimes Kṛṣṇa-viddhi Prabhu goes alone) to world-famous Oxford Street, and every Saturday night there is a *mahā-harināma* around London’s West End. Sometimes we hold long *kīrtana* sessions in the temple room under the direction and care of our GBC, your dear disciple His Holiness Śivarāma Swami. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, following in the footsteps of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, you taught us to chant the holy names accompanied by musical instruments and dancing. We offer our respectful obeisances unto you and beg for your mercy so that we may maintain and increase our chanting, so we may eventually attain offenseless chanting.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you personally invited Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara to reside here; then you engaged us in daily dressing, feeding, and worshiping Them exquisitely, keeping the temple spotlessly clean, welcoming Their guests and visitors, and so on. We offer our respectful obeisances unto you and beg for your mercy so that we may increase and improve the quality of our service to Their Lordships and Their guests and visitors.

Our Govinda’s Restaurant, headed by Mother Govinda-nandinī and her husband, Śuddhātmā Prabhu, served 90,000 customers last year (compared to 75,000 the previous year). Our Food for Life team, led by Bhaktas Dilraj, Duncan, and Chris Godbold, distributed 13,000 plates of *prasādam* to the homeless and others (and have done so consistently the past four years). Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you taught us how to cook four kinds of delicious foods to offer to Kṛṣṇa. You also instructed us on the benefits of honoring *kṛṣṇa-prasādam*. We offer our respectful obeisances unto you and beg for your mercy so that we may improve our cooking and our attitude toward *prasādam,* and to maintain and expand our *prasādam* distribution in Central London.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are always eager to preach about Kṛṣṇa. By your example, we continue to have daily *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* classes, lunchtime lectures, and weekly Sunday Feast programs. We offer our respectful obeisances unto you and beg for your mercy so that we may develop a genuine taste for hearing and sincerely speaking about the qualities, names, pastimes, forms, and devotees of Śrī Rādhikā and Mādhava, so that those who listen may be attracted to Them.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your expert and tasteful arrangements, the pastimes of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, Girirāja, Jagannātha, Baladeva, Subhadra, and Gaura-Nitāi have become manifest before our eyes. We offer our respectful obeisances unto you and beg for your mercy so that we can see these pastimes as actual pastimes and not mere festivals; we maintain such arrangements for your pleasure and that of Their Lordships.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, your own personal qualities, activities, teachings, and faithful dedication in fulfilling your spiritual master’s orders have clearly shown you to be a highly exemplary disciple and a most rare and bona-fide Vaiṣṇava spiritual master. We offer our respectful obeisances unto you and beg for your mercy so that we may become similarly faithful and competent in fulfilling your orders.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in our endeavors to maintain and develop your London temple, we sometimes experience Himalayan levels of anxiety! Yet we keep on because you informed us how dearly your Guru Mahārāja wanted this temple, and we know how pleased you became when your *gṛhastha* disciples successfully started it. We keep on because we have faith that it makes you happy to know that this temple is here in the heart of one of the most famous cities in the world. We keep on despite sometimes wanting to give up, because we aspire for your mercy. We trust that by that mercy, Kṛṣṇa will be pleased with us.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your servants at the ISKCON temple in the Soho district of London, UK.*  
(written by Māyāpur dāsa)

## London (south)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace, the *senapati-bhakta* who fearlessly carried the message of Śrī Caitanya to the whole world!

Indeed, you left your beloved Vṛndāvana at the age of sixty-nine, when most people retire, to preach the holy name of Hari to the Western world. You performed this formidable task in places where practically everyone is addicted to sinful life. Over the next eleven years, you wrote over seventy books and traveled the world fourteen times, opening temples and initiating and guiding devotees. You had taken the instruction of your Guru Mahārāja to heart: to preach in English and to print books.

Your books are the foundation of this movement, allowing countless souls wandering in this world to reach the eternal and ever-increasing bliss of the spiritual world, the abode of Śrī Kṛṣṇa and Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī. You said that these books are the law books for the next ten thousand years, the Golden Age, and that you will live forever in your books. Prabhupāda, you were undeniably repeating the words of Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, who wrote:

He reasons ill who tells that Vaiṣṇavas die  
When thou art living still in sound.  
The Vaiṣṇavas die to live and living try  
To spread the holy life around!

You glorified the Lord by spreading His holy name throughout the world, while Śrī Kṛṣṇa glorifies you as His pure devotee. As a true disciple of a bona fide spiritual master, you attributed all your success to Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. You thus displayed your devout humility.

Your only interest lies in how to please Kṛṣṇa by pleasing His devotees. You are always prepared to engage everyone else in this service. You are an eternally liberated soul, whose only business in this world is to get us out of this cycle of repeated birth and death. You magnanimously showed us the way back home, back to Godhead.

Yet you always speak boldly and truthfully, never diluting the prescription by which we can get rid of our skin disease. Out of mercy, you tolerate our offenses; you love us despite our faults, since it’s only through being loved that we can use our tiny independence in the service of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

We pray to you that we may be able to develop Vaiṣṇava qualities and become suitable recipients for the blessings of Śrī Kṛṣṇa and His devotees. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we will never be able to repay our debt of gratitude to you, and it’s nice to be forever indebted to you.

Jagat Guru Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

*Your grandchildren at ISKCON’s temple in South London, UK.*  
(written by Gomatī-devī dāsī)

## Longdenville

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*nāma-śreṣṭhaṁ manum api śacī-putram atra svarūpaṁ*  
*rūpaṁ tasyāgrajam uru-purīṁ māthurīṁ goṣṭhavāṭīm*  
*rādha-kuṇḍaṁ giri-varam aho rādhikā-mādhavāśāṁ*  
*prāpto yasya prathita-kṛpayā śrī-guruṁ taṁ nato ’smi*

**“I bow down to the beautiful lotus feet of my spiritual master, by whose causeless mercy I have obtained the supreme holy name, the divine mantra, the service of the son of Śacī-mātā, the association of Śrīla Svarūpa Dāmodara, Rūpa Gosvāmī, and his older brother Sanātana Gosvāmī, the supreme abode of Mathurā, the blissful abode of Vṛndāvana, the divine Rādhā-kuṇḍa and Govardhana Hill, and the desire within my heart for the loving service of Śrī Rādhikā and Mādhava in Vṛndāvana.”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your supremely worshipable lotus feet. Unlimited glories to you on this, the most wonderful anniversary of your appearance in this world!

On Nandotsava, just to celebrate the wonderful appearance of Lord Kṛṣṇa, Nanda Mahārāja gave thousands of cows and valuable items in charity, but five thousand years later, on the anniversary of this celebration of Nandotsava, it was Kṛṣṇa Himself who gave gifts. By Kṛṣṇa’s causeless mercy, on September 1st, 1896, we were given the association of His own dearmost servant, our beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda. Your appearance was not forced like we are forced to take birth in this material world. Indeed you have appeared under the divine order of Kṛṣṇa Himself. It is not within the capacity of conditioned souls like ourselves to properly glorify you, but taking clumps of straw within our mouths we offer this Vyasa-puja homage to you on the most auspicious occasion of your appearance in this world.

The desire of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa, is to give pure love of Himself to all the living entities who have entered this material universe by misusing their minute independence. Indeed, fulfilling this desire is the sum and substance of all the revealed scriptures. This message was first passed to Brahmājī at the beginning of creation and was very carefully handed down through the chain of spiritual masters and disciples until something very wonderful happened—Śrīla Mādhavendra Purī manifested the ripened fruit of the tree of love of Godhead in this world and made ready the coming of that Godhead Himself in the form of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

In Śrīmān Mahāprabhu came the tree of love of Godhead and the cultivator and maintainer of the tree itself. But Caitanya Mahāprabhu did not make this very rare fruit available for a select few. He did not withhold the fruits of that tree from anyone. Indeed, He gave the fruit to whomever He met, and His devotees also made the nectarean fruit of love of Kṛṣṇa available to whomever they met. In such a spirit they flooded the entire land with ecstatic love for Kṛṣṇa, the darling son of Nanda and Yaśodā.

Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu then initiated His plan to distribute the fruits of that tree to the entire earth planet, divinely empowering His own dear servant Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī to establish that mission in this world. And from Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī this desire came to Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, who envisioned that very soon a greatly empowered devotee would appear in order to fulfill Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s order that in every town and village the names of Kṛṣṇa would be heard and chanted. And he envisioned a great city in the land of Māyāpur where Vaiṣṇava devotees of the Lord would come from all over the world to delight in the wonderful nectar emanating from the holy name of Kṛṣṇa.

The preaching spirit of Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura entered into the heart of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, a lion among preachers and a terror in the hearts of the Māyāvādīs who would dare say that Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Godhead, has no form. Indeed, all so-called devotees and pseudo-spiritualists would cower in fear when the thunderous voice of the *siṁha-guru* would boldly declare that Kṛṣṇa, the beloved of the residents of Vraja, was the only object of all affection.

It was he, that lion among Vaiṣṇavas, who recognized one Abhay Charan De on the rooftop of an *āśrama* on Ultadanga Road: “You are an educated young man. Why don’t you preach the message of Lord Caitanya throughout the whole world?” Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura laid to rest any argument you presented to test him, and into your heart he firmly established the desire to spread the mission of Śrīmān Mahāprabhu. Later you remarked:

I was from a Vaiṣṇava family, so I could appreciate what he was preaching. Of course, he was speaking to everyone, but he found something in me. And I was convinced about his argument and mode of presentation. I was so much struck with wonder. I could understand: Here is the proper person who can give a real religious idea. \[*Śrīla Prabhupāda Līlāmṛta:* “A Very Nice Saintly Person”\]

Taking those instructions of your Guru Mahārāja to heart, you set out to offer that service to his lotus feet, spreading the mission of Lord Caitanya throughout the world. This fruit was entrusted to you just as it was entrusted to Brahmājī at the beginning of creation, and just as Lord Caitanya Himself entrusted it to Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī. Now the fruit of the desire tree of devotional service was in your firm grasp.

How glorious you are for giving this fruit to the whole world! How dear you are to Kṛṣṇa for having spread His glories throughout the entire world! Kṛṣṇa did not do it; He simple spoke the essential philosophy at Kurukṣetra. Caitanya Mahāprabhu did not do it: He remained in the confines of India. No other person did it. But you did it! You released the floodgates and let loose this nectar of the holy name. Indeed, there is no other person who has accomplished what you have done.

Not only did you give us this most valuable gift, but you also carefully taught us how to care for it properly. By your example you perfectly taught us how to be proper devotees of Kṛṣṇa.

By your ferocious efforts, that wonderful city envisioned by Ṭhākura Bhaktivinoda is being established in the land of Māyāpur, and every year countless Vaiṣṇavas, devotees of Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu, flock there to chant the holy name in ecstatic love. How dear you must be to Ṭhākura Bhaktivinoda!

O how glorious you are that the mercy of Caitanya Mahāprabhu flows in you, empowering you to wonderfully present your *bhāṣya* on the beautiful *Bhāgavatam,* thereby giving proper understanding of Kṛṣṇa to unlimited fallen souls. How powerful you are to have cracked the stonelike hearts of us *mlecchas* and lowborn men so that we would become Vaiṣṇavas, devotees of Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu! How merciful you are to have saved us from the deep, dark recess of material illusion and given us Kṛṣṇa consciousness, whereby we can revive our pure love for that most beautiful of all beautiful persons, Śyāmasundara.

Your glories, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are as innumerable as the particles of dust in the whole universe. It is very difficult for even the greatest of poets to properly sing your praises, for you are an unlimited ocean of wonderful qualities.
