How Kṛṣṇa must be so pleased with your service, and how satisfied Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī must be with you! You started your mission very humbly, and it has grown to a worldwide organization of temples, farms, schools, and other venues for spreading the glories of Kṛṣṇa and giving the mercy of Lord Caitanya to one and all.

Indeed, you have perfectly fulfilled the desire of Lord Caitanya and firmly established His mission in this world! You perfectly demonstrated how to capture the mercy of the Lord by spreading His glories.

Now we, your humble servants and the servants of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha Mandir, re-dedicate ourselves to your mission and sincerely pray for your mercy so that we may always represent you properly and always be guided by you in our efforts to render pleasing service to Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the darling son of Nanda and Yaśodā. Please give us the benediction that we may always aspire to be the humble servants of the servants of your devotees. No other benediction is useful to us. Please always shelter us under the shade of your lotus feet. You are the savior of the most fallen!

We hope that we will one day be able to serve you without motive.

*Your insignificant servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha Mandir in Longdenville, Trinidad.*  
(written by Śānta Vigraha dāsa)

## Malaga

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Today is the most glorious anniversary of your appearance in this material world. We are eternally indebted to you and the ISKCON movement you have created. You are the *cintāmaṇi* gem who has turned the most fallen *śūdras* into first-class *brāhmaṇas* through the medium of the holy scriptures that you so painstakingly translated. Undergoing severe austerities, you rendered them into common language within the grasp of ordinary people.

Not only foreigners but also Indians, who you rightly said are naturally God conscious, have benefited immensely from your movement. Many have now realized the difference between the one and only Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and His associates, His innumerable incarnations, and His expansions in the form of the variegated demigods.

We were like dogs in the streets without a master, not knowing which direction to take and under whose shelter to seek safety. By taking shelter of your movement, we now feel safe and secure and have found our life’s goal, which is to always remember Kṛṣṇa and never forget Him even for a second. Your mission has spread throughout the whole world and is continuing to grow more and more powerful with each day because you are still present with us—in our hearts, in our minds, and in your books. Truly a Vaiṣṇava never dies. He lives forever!

Your mercy is most visible through your disciples—your representatives and “soldiers,” as you rightly called them. Recently we personally witnessed the magnitude of such causeless mercy. How else but through the association of your most beloved disciples could an ordinary householder, at the age of seventy, who never saw you personally or read your books, rise up to the stage of getting totally immersed in the nectar-ocean of the holy name and chanting sixty-four rounds daily, without fail, and thereby finally leave his body in such a glorious way that we are sure he must have returned to your lotus feet?

The chain of disciplic succession must go on. Just as you worked so hard to accomplish the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu—spreading His holy name to every town and village—so all of us devotees should accept as our life’s mission the bringing of more and more living entities to your lotus feet. We will succeed only if we have complete faith in your words, just as you had complete faith in the words of your Guru Mahārāja.

We most humbly fold our hands and beg for your mercy and blessings so that we may be your pawns on this gigantic chessboard of the material world and work harmoniously in spreading love of God to all the fallen souls. We wish to bring them to your lotus feet so you can take them back to their original home, back to Godhead, for an eternal life of service at the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.

*Your humble servants at the ISKCON temple in Malaga, Spain.*

## Mangalore

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you!

Your temple here at the hometown of Śrīpāda Madhvācārya is heralding the dawn of a new era. After being here for about ten years, ISKCON Mangalore is now beginning to gain a firm footing. In the last year a lot of new devotees have taken an interest in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and book distribution is increasing quietly.

Being one of the most educated areas in the world, and being the home of a very large number of Madhva *brāhmaṇas,* this place is *the* place for your books. We can see from the feedback we receive that people who have read them with even a little interest are becoming devotees. Throughout this region, from Mangalore to Udupi, we have small groups of devotees preaching according to their capacity.

We hope that in the coming years we will be able to fulfill the desires of Your Divine Grace, which are identical to those of Ācārya Madhva.

*Your eternal servants at ISKCON Mangalore, India.*

## Manila

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and prostrated obeisances to your divine lotus feet.

Today, on your Vyāsa-pūjā day, we come together to reflect on your great and wonderful appearance in this earthly world. It is so great indeed because you single-handedly transformed millions upon millions of fallen conditioned souls into devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa and start them on their journey back to Godhead. And what makes it more wonderful is that you continue to do so with the transcendental books you gave us. Time and time again someone comes to the temple and reveals that he or she has become attracted to ISKCON because of coming in contact with your books, with your devotees, or with something else connected to ISKCON. And the most mystical part of it all is that although you are not physically present among us, the mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness is going on unchecked and unstoppable because of the spiritual power you left us with.

For our part, we have no good qualities. Before becoming devotees we led a life full of sin. Indeed, our path was one of animalistic life. There was not much hope for us, for there was nothing good about how we lived our lives. Despite all these disqualifications, something magical happened when we came to know you: we started to behave like human beings. But we confess that even now, though we are leading a spiritual life, our hearts still need a lot of cleansing. We still think of how to enjoy sense pleasure and run after Māyā. We therefore submit ourselves to you on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā and pray fervently that you kindly give us the necessary strength to continue as ISKCON devotees and become worthy of being accepted as your grand-disciples. Please make us suitable instruments in assisting you and our spiritual masters in spreading the legacy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s Vyāsa-pūjā *kī jaya*!

*Your menial servants in Manila, Philippines.*  
(written by Śrīdāmā dāsa)

## Miami

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you!

You once said that the purpose of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is to make us individually thoughtful. That is very significant because it means that each of us is personally and individually accountable for our own actions. No one can prevent us from becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious, and no one can become Kṛṣṇa conscious for us.

We will know we are advancing in Kṛṣṇa consciousness when we become humble enough to accept that you have given us everything we need to become pure devotees of the Lord, and that all your instructions are applicable to our own lives. In the early years of the movement, we often wondered why you so heavily criticized the Māyāvādīs and the concept of impersonalism. Gradually we understood that you were actually preaching to us, who are so much influenced by impersonalism and voidism.

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

You would boldly preach against the cheating mentality of the materialists. You explained that materialists minimize the position of God, even to the point of totally denying His existence. They think that by denying the existence of God they will not be held accountable for their actions. You pointed out, however, that God is not tricked by their cheating mentality and still holds them accountable by forcing them to take birth as trees. Trees have such low consciousness that they can tolerate standing for thousands of years, and even if a tree is cut down, it has no ability to object. With such covered consciousness, it is impossible to realize God. That is the ultimate punishment.

You preached strongly against the materialists’ cheating mentality for our benefit, because we are still more or less influenced by that mentality. By strictly following your instructions, we will be protected from that influence and will gradually become free from all material contamination. But to the degree that we try to maintain our materialistic cheating mentality, there will be a reaction. The reaction is that our consciousness will become more covered and we will become further removed from becoming pure devotees of the Lord.

Every one of us, according to our position in ISKCON, has a certain degree of accountability. During your physical presence you demanded that all your disciples be fully accountable for their actions, from chanting sixteen rounds daily and following the four regulative principles to properly executing higher responsibilities as a temple president, GBC secretary, or a *sannyāsī*. If we sincerely followed your instructions, we advanced in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. If we did not, our Kṛṣṇa consciousness waned. Now, in your physical absence, you are holding us no less accountable for our actions than before. Unfortunately, as a materialist may feel himself free from personal accountability to God because he does not see God before him, so we may feel ourselves free from accountability to you, Kṛṣṇa’s pure representative, because we do not see you here to guide us and chastise us.

The fact is that you are still with us and helping us progress in spiritual life. If we sincerely want to advance in spiritual life, you will help us all the way. If however, we choose to develop and maintain a cheating mentality, acting externally as devotees but internally maintaining ulterior motives, we will not advance. It is not possible to remain in a stagnant position, so if we are not becoming more Kṛṣṇa conscious, then our Kṛṣṇa consciousness is waning. This principle applies to everyone, regardless of our external position or the amount of service we have rendered within this movement.

Lord Kṛṣṇa is constantly testing us to help us monitor our own Kṛṣṇa consciousness. He will never give us a test too difficult to pass, but that does not mean we have to face those tests alone. You said that if one thinks that he can make spiritual advancement outside the association of devotees, he is living in a great hallucination. Ultimately, our real test is to see how well we will cooperate in your ISKCON to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness everywhere. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please continue guiding us on the path of Kṛṣṇa consciousness so that we can perfect our lives and become your effective instruments in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world.

*Your humble servants at the ISKCON temple in Miami, Florida, USA.*

## Montreal

**“Now that Lord Caitanya, whose heart is overflowing with mercy, has incarnated within this world, those living entities who had formerly never practiced yoga, meditated, chanted mantras, performed austerities, followed various Vedic restrictions, studied the** ***Vedas,*** **acted in a saintly manner, or refrained from committing sins are able to plunder with great delight pure love of God, which is the crest jewel of all human endeavors.” (*****Śrī Caitanya-candrāmṛta*** **111)**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

To celebrate your appearance day is to celebrate a pivotal moment in the history of mankind. It was in the year of your birth that Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura reached out to the West by sending his work *Śrī Gaurāṅga-līlā-smaraṇa-maṅgala-stotra* to various Western university libraries and intellectuals of the time. It was the first time that English Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava literature had reached Western shores. Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura thus planted the seed that would blossom into your worldwide mission to fulfill Lord Caitanya’s prophecy: “In as many towns and villages as there are on the surface of the earth, My holy name will be preached.” Who could have known then that you incarnated the preaching spirit of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu for our modern times? Who knew then that, armed only with the instruction of Śrī Guru, you would set about constructing “a house in which everyone can live”? Who could have known then that on that auspicious day there had appeared that rare soul Lord Kṛṣṇa describes in the *Bhagavad-gītā* (3.21): “Whatever action a great man performs, common men follow. And whatever standards he sets by exemplary acts, all the world pursues”?

Later, as you were leaving this mundane world, your earthly mission accomplished, you comforted your apprehensive and turbulent disciples by telling them:

Śrīla Prabhupāda \[Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī\] said that we should preach in Europe, America. That was his desire. And his other desire was that we all would work together jointly to preach. I didn’t waste a single moment. I tried my best, and it has been successful to some extent. If we work conjointly, then, as Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu said, *pṛthivīte*...*saṅkīrtana* has great possibilities. Do not fight among yourselves. I have given direction in my books.

On this, your glorious appearance day, we pray that we, your disciples and followers, may all work together to carry your legacy to the suffering masses of humanity. Bless us with your mercy that we might disregard the foolish quest for name, fame, and material gain. We cannot even begin to estimate the many blessings we already enjoy within the confines of this mortal world due to your infinite mercy, what to speak, then, of the great hope that now enlightens our hearts, the hope that someday soon we shall be liberated from this world of birth, disease, old age, and death and eternally engage in the loving service of the lotus feet of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. May our gratitude to you be shown by our eagerness to drown out the crocodiles of dissension lurking within our hearts and by our efforts to promote incessantly and enthusiastically your mission to the world.

“Unity in diversity” you taught as the means of conquering over the wiles of Kali. Please let not our awkward effort at following in your giant footsteps be adulterated, either in its unity or in its diversity, by the two irreparable faults of mental speculation and sense gratification: “Devotional service of the Lord that ignores the authorized Vedic literatures like the *Upaniṣads, Purāṇas, Nārada Pañcarātra,* etc., is simply an unnecessary disturbance in society.” This we ask knowing only too well that to celebrate your appearance properly we must satisfy you in every way, both collectively and individually. Where else but to your lotus feet shall we turn for shelter in this world of fearful implication? How else can we ever hope to please Lord Kṛṣṇa? How are we to ever realize the true meaning of chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare? Did you not yourself confirm both in life and word what all the scriptures proclaim loudly: The satisfaction of the bona fide spiritual master makes all things possible?

*Your servants at the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Manohara, and Śrī Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā in Montreal, Canada.*

## Mumbai (Chowpatty)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most prostrated obeisances unto your divine lotus feet. We at ISKCON’s Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha Mandir feel ourselves most fortunate to have found shelter at your most merciful lotus feet. Thank you for your causeless mercy.

Often we have heard you quoted as saying that Mumbai is your office. As your followers, we have realized that to understand and implement the purport this statement would greatly please you. It could also result in your showering on us your mercy.

For your pleasure, we would like to present to you how wonderfully the preaching efforts in Mumbai are developing. The youth in Mumbai are taking to Kṛṣṇa consciousness very seriously and in large numbers. Last year we started a series of monthly youth festivals titled “Prerana.” The festival now attracts anywhere between eight hundred and a thousand young people in the 15–30 age from all over Mumbai, for a Kṛṣṇa conscious evening of chanting, dancing, philosophy, and feasting. The festival has received much favorable coverage in the local media.

Also, you may be pleased to know that the children in our Vaiṣṇava community now have a better facility to receive an education based on Kṛṣṇa conscious principles. In our school—called Gopal’s Garden—the syllabus is designed to keep Kṛṣṇa always in the center. The students appear so happy and contented. Along with participating in devotional plays, picnics, and *yātrās,* they learn the three R’s.

The temple has been transformed from a simple building into on of Mumbai’s most beautiful landmark. With ornate sandstone carvings on the outside and beautiful, intricate, delicate woodwork on the inside, the temple is attracting hundreds of visitors every day, and they all get the opportunity to have the wonderful *darśana* of the Deities. They also hear about and discuss Kṛṣṇa consciousness and, finally, carry away with them your precious books. The worldwide congregation of Vaiṣṇavas is kindly giving us opportunities to serve them in many wonderful ways. We were particularly blessed to serve your devotees in Vṛndāvana on the occasion of the celebrations honoring the twenty-fifth anniversary of you disappearance day celebrations, and also on the twenty-fifth anniversary of the opening of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsabihārī Mandir at Hare Krishna Land, Juhu. Also, at our temple you are kindly blessing us by allowing us the opportunity to serve so many of your most dear disciples and devotees. The devotees very much appreciate our feeble attempts to look to their comforts. Thus we are doubly blessed—by you and by your devotees.

On festivals like Janmāṣṭamī, Rādhāṣṭamī, Vyāsa-pūjā, Gaura Pūrṇimā, and especially Śarad Pūrṇimā, thousands of devotees come together to glorify the Supreme Lord and His consort. Being merciful on us, the Lord has arranged that now we have unique dioramas for each festival. On Gaura Pūrṇimā the Pañca-Tattva arrived to bless us, and for Rāma Navamī we had the Rāma Darbar. The opportunities for serving the Lord are thus expanding more and more.

The congregation has increased greatly over the past year, and along with that increase has developed the counselor system for proper care and welfare of devotees. This system is very effective, and the devotees in the congregation feel well cared for. Out of their heartfelt, overflowing love for you and your devotees they are dedicating themselves wholeheartedly to the preaching mission.

Book distribution has been going on steadily, and last year during the December marathon we again distributed many of your books. Worldwide we stood third. Our desire is to simply go on increasing this program of book distribution.

Thus, in so many ways you have been kind enough to allow us to assist you in your ISKCON mission in your office, Mumbai.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we thank you again and again for so mercifully engaging us. We sincerely pray to be always engaged in your services simply for your pleasure.

Please bless us that we may never become proud. Please bless us to never consider ourselves the doers. Also, kindly bless us to always remain the humble servants of the servants of your servants and thus constantly chant the holy names—Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

*Your humble servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha Mandir in the Chowpatty section of Mumbai, India.*

## Mumbai (Juhu)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

The devotees of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsabihārījī Mandir, your favorite transcendental “office,” were greatly pleased to celebrate its Silver Jubilee on January 14, 2003. We invited your dear servants who set up this wonderful project to come and join us in a Festival of Reunion. Many of them accepted the invitation and came. They described your transcendental pastimes in establishing this temple, and their memories deeply touched our hearts. We were simply drowned in the nectar of your wonderful activities. Our hearts melted upon hearing the difficulties you underwent to establish this center. By understanding a fraction of your vision for this project, we have renewed our determination to dedicate ourselves to it.

On the occasion of the Silver Jubilee, we also laid the foundation stone for the second phase of your project. With the help of the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsabihārījī Committee members, we are attempting to realize your dream of making Hare Krishna Land “Heaven on Earth.”

We plan to move your Bhaktivedanta Swami Mission School from Hare Krishna Land to a new site at Sastri Nagar, in Andheri West. The school will thus have additional facilities, and in Juhu we will have a larger space for our spiritual educational programs and other preaching activities.

We need to make many significant decisions for these two projects, namely “Heaven on Earth” and the school, such as allocation of space for the various facilities, the project design and its execution, and fund-raising. We are depending completely on your mercy to make the right decisions. Please bless us to be an instrument in your grand preaching plans in this most important city of Mumbai, which is the “Gateway of India.”

Aspiring to be always engaged in your service,

*Your servants at the lotus feet of your beloved Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsabihārījī, in the Juhu section of Mumbai, India.*

## Nagpur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

It is our great fortune that we have the opportunity to be a part of a mission being led by a true, sincere, and pure devotee of Kṛṣṇa like you. It is only because of you that we have taken to the path of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. It is because of your compassion and mercy that the people of the world are becoming lovers of Kṛṣṇa today.

Out of your many temples and centers across the world, we are a small center in Nagpur. We are trying to preach and expand your mission here. We know how much you had to go through in your life to establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness globally, and you set for us a perfect example through your struggles. You are our inspiration, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Last time we told you that we were trying to get a proper plot of land for ISKCON Nagpur. This time we have a good news for you: we have now acquired it. This is the fruit of your blessings. Now that we have the land, the real struggle has begun. We don’t think we have any capacity of our own, and so we totally depend on you. We pray to you to please send nice devotees to Nagpur who will help magnify your glorious preaching movement.

On this most auspicious day, we pray at your feet to bless us so that we may never forget you, even for a moment, and pray in the same way at the feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha.

*Your humble servants at ISKCON Nagpur, India.*

## New Bahulavan

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept the most humble obeisances of all the devotees in New Bhahulavan through the mercy of our Gurudevas.

More than five hundred years after Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s appearance, but just a short time after my joining this movement, I feel honored to represent all the devotees in New Bahulavan in offering our most sincere yet most insignificant prayers on this most auspicious day—the appearance day of Your Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda.

Your physical existence in this world has remained permanently in the hearts of those who were fortunate enough to know ISKCON more than twenty-five years ago, and your brilliant achievements and immortal spirit will remain forever with all of us.

More than a hundred years ago, a great soul appeared on this planet, as foretold by Lord Caitanya’s prophecy: “Lord Kṛṣṇa’s holy name will be spread to every village and town all over the world.”

The Lord predicted that the seeds of *bhakti* would take root throughout the world. And within thirty-eight short years after you so courageously left India for the West, these seeds have been spread all over the world, and the flourishing garden of *bhakti* is now thriving worldwide. Khasadeśa has begun its real life by your mercy. You sent brilliant and devoted spiritual masters to dig up the wild soil in this vast land and plant those *bhakti-bījas,* and during the last ten years, trees, birds, and flowers have appeared everywhere.

Although there are still wind, rain, thunderbolts, extreme heat, and bitter cold that assault the pure atmosphere in this garden of devotion, nothing can stop the melting warmth of the firm faith and pure love that our spiritual masters have for the Lord.

You have set a brilliant example for all those who are fortunate enough to come to the devotional path. In this beautiful city of New Tālavan, the seeds of devotion are now quietly sprouting and the garden is growing luxuriant under your endless protection.

On this most auspicious and most holy day, we, the devotees in New Bhahulavan, respectfully offer our prayers to you,

A person who can move our hearts,

A great saint who has sacrificed everything for the Lord,

An *ācārya* who lives by his own example.

You live in your instructions, and your instructions illuminate our path on our way back home. Your instructions destroy Māyā’s powerful shackles. Your instructions create a devotional planet.

*Your most humble servants in New Bahulavan, Khasadeśa.*

## New Biharvan

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you and Lord Caitanya, in whose army you serve as the Senapati Commander to propagate the holy name throughout this planet!

We are writing this offering the day after Rāma Navamī. During the festival, we were reflecting on the striking resemblance between what Śrī Hanumān did for Lord Rāmacandra and what you did for Lord Caitanya. Both of you were commanders in the Lord’s army, ready to execute His will.

Hanumān crossed the ocean to rescue Mother Sītā and fight the demon Rāvaṇa and his party of demons. While crossing, he displayed intelligence, courage, and determination, as he was confronted by Maināka Mountain, Sūrṣā, and Siṁhikā. Similarly, you said that your mission was to reunite Sītā (Lakṣmī) with Rāma by using the material opulence of the West in Kṛṣṇa’s service. For this purpose, you also crossed the ocean and were faced with seasickness and two heart attacks

But Śrī Hanumān was empowered by Lord Rāma. He found Mother Sītā and fearlessly faced the demon King Rāvaṇa alone, even though he was bound with ropes. You also faced atheists, impersonalists, and the harsh New York winters alone.

The whole world was amazed when an army of monkeys and bears defeated the greatest demons of the time. Similarly, the world is amazed by the success of your simple “hippie” followers in bringing Kṛṣṇa consciousness to a hedonistic world. Hanumānjī can be credited with establishing Rāma-rājya in Laṅkā. Similarly, you have established the *nāma-haṭṭa* (market place of the holy name) throughout the whole world.

When Lord Rāma left this planet to return to His eternal abode, Saketā, He requested Hanumānjī to stay behind. Hanumān is always present wherever the name of Lord Rāma is chanted. We think that Lord Caitanya and Lord Nityānanda similarly left you to propagate the chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we feel your guiding presence in Boise every day.

Last year, you were present when we held our first full-fledged Ratha-yātrā, with a beautiful chariot made by members of the Boise temple. Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā-devī graced the Fourth of July parade along with you. At the start of the parade, the Idaho National Air Guard jets dipped down in formation right before the chariot, as if to pay their respects. Devotees from Portland and Boise danced and distributed *prasādam* in front of the *ratha*.

Every year we start the New Year with a Japathon, in which we chant the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* on beads continuously for eight hours. This year, we collectively chanted more than 1.5 million holy names, and we all felt that you were chanting with us. During the summer we read “Planting the Seed” every Saturday, and Michelle Bird received initiation from His Holiness Gopāl Kṛṣṇa Mahārāja, becoming Mādhurya-Rādhā-devī dāsī. On Dīpāvalī day, the Sunday school children enacted the entire *Sundara Kāṇḍa* of the *Rāmāyaṇa* for your pleasure.

Your books are being distributed regularly at Boise State University. Professors, students, and interfaith clergy continue to marvel at your powerful purports. Next week we will finish a detailed study of *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is*. A dedicated band of twenty *bhaktas* and *bhaktins* have met every Friday evening for almost two years to try and understand Kṛṣṇa’s words through your purports.

All of this is due to your divine presence at the Boise temple. Please continue to lead us across the ocean of *saṁsāra*. Your mercy is all that we are made of.

*Your foolish monkeys in the forest of New Biharvan, in Boise, Idaho, USA.*  
(written by Anantarūpa dāsa)

## New Dvārakā

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Today I received the honor of writing this *Vyāsa-Pūjā* offering. It was on this day, almost thirty years ago, that you accepted me as your disciple. That was the happiest day of my life. I remember the first time I saw your photograph. I had just finished reading your small pamphlet *Transcendental Teachings of Prahlāda Mahārāja*, and I was thinking, “Now here is the bona fide spiritual master.” My attraction to you was “love at first sight.” Later, I began reading your *Kṛṣṇa* book, and when I read the chapter where Kṛṣṇa kills Pūtanā, I decided that I must join your movement. It took a few months more to do that, but your Brooklyn temple accepted me, and your wonderful disciples taught me how to execute Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Currently, I am in Los Angeles, and your disciples and grand-disciples are continuing my training. One of your grand-disciples sums up how I feel about your movement succinctly: “My best day in the material world doesn’t even compare with my worst day in ISKCON.”

I am so happy to be a part of your movement. I have had the experience of living in and out of the temple, but nothing beats the association of devotees. Everything about your movement is sublime: the chanting, the Deity worship, the festivals, the *prasādam,* and the wonderful devotees. But your books are the most important. Without your purports, the other items would not be understood rightly. There is something special about the way you have composed your books.

Over the years, some of your disciples have left your movement, being allured by the illusion of “greener grass,” and some have even tried to tempt me to become inclined toward other Vaiṣṇavas. I ventured to read some of their books, but something was seriously lacking. What I have come to understand is that I love you and want to be like you, because you are the greatest person I have ever met in my life. I can never forget you, even though I hardly had any close or direct association with you. Somehow, by being in your movement and reading your books, I have become completely attached to you. No one can replace you in my life. After all is said and done, there is really only one, and that one is you, my eternal spiritual master. You have instilled in your disciples the fundamental teaching of Lord Caitanya—that it is the combined mercy of *guru* and Kṛṣṇa that fosters the seed of devotion in the heart. It is a great mistake to try to have one without the other.

Since joining your camp, I have seen the words of your purports come to life, one after the other. It never ceases to amaze me how you so expertly and accurately know what is going on in the little world of the conditioned soul. Some of your purports are so astounding because you pinpoint my faulty consciousness in every way, every time. And even more amazing is that some of your disciples cannot accept some of your teachings on the plea of political correctness and modernization. I stay away from such disciples, fearing that they are displeasing to you.

You have taught that the best way to please you is by preaching, just as you set the perfect example in this regard yourself. No one can ever touch your legacy, though imposters may try by bluffing and fooling less-intelligent, uninformed neophytes, who are innocent but unfortunate victims. Sometimes there is much diligent debate and discussion about how to preach. But my experience dictates that there is nothing bolder than just reading your purports out loud, as they are, and explaining them line by line in plain and simple language. I can never forget what you wrote in your *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (my favorite book of yours):

One must be an authorized Vaiṣṇava, humble and pure. One should write transcendental literature to purify oneself, not for credit. By writing about the pastimes of the Lord, one associates with the Lord directly. One should not ambitiously think, “I shall become a great author. I shall be celebrated as a writer.” These are material desires. One should attempt to write for self-purification. It may be published or it may not be published, but that does not matter. If one is actually sincere in writing, all his ambitions will be fulfilled. Whether one is known as a great author is incidental. One should not attempt to write transcendental literature for material name and fame.”

I apply this same logic when I preach.

Over the years, my wife and I have composed some poems in glorification of you.

my life had been a game  
i wanted it to end  
but life began again  
the day you changed my name  
Śrīla Prabhupāda sacrificed his life  
to save this earthly planet  
from famine, war and strife  
remember all the troubles  
and pains he undertook  
so if you want to help him  
come out and sell his...  
***Books****,* ***Books***  
Śrīla Prabhupāda’s ***Books***  
in every town and village  
all around the world  
if you really want to please him  
then just distribute ***Books***  
a devotee of *Kṛṣṇa*  
the savior of the human race  
he came as a messenger of the Lord  
unaware of the hardships he’d face  
with but very few possessions  
he set sail for the Western World  
to fulfill the order of *Guru*  
was his duty, a calling from the Divine  
as he traveled for many days  
until he reached the Boston Pier  
he wrote down in his diary  
“My Lord *Kṛṣṇa*, why have You brought me here?”  
after struggling alone  
*Kṛṣṇa* sent him many followers  
to help him in his mission  
of printing books on *Kṛṣṇa* consciousness  
*Kṛṣṇa* is GOD!  
so let us tell the world  
by preaching the knowledge he gave to us  
we’ll be purified  
you came to us in ‘65  
and from above you gave us love  
into the dark you sent us light  
simply so we could do things right  
but now you are gone  
and we struggle on  
still we miss you  
*Prabhupāda*  
*Prabhupāda*, come again please  
here we are on our knees begging if you please  
and until we’re back to Godhead  
we pray and pray more each day  
’cause we love you  
*Prabhupāda*  
Prabhupāda kindly show the way  
*Please* help this soul who suffers this day  
at every moment I just meet defeat  
kindly give me shelter at your lotus feet  
you’re teaching me to be pure and wise  
*Please* come and force open my closed eyes  
give me shelter i have no where to turn  
pure love of ***Kṛṣṇa*** I desire to earn  
i’m such a fool i cannot see  
all the things you have given me  
you’ve taught all there is to know  
but my acceptance is very slow  
i pray to you with all my heart  
to let me have another start  
so undeserving i know i am  
without your help i’m surely damned  
*Please* give me just one more chance  
and like a puppet make me dance  
in anyway that you so desire  
just put out the blazing fire  
you taught us everything we know  
because you are so kind  
you came to relieve us of all distress  
and purify our mind  
so many troubles you had to face  
to bring us love of God  
your kindness overwhelmed us all  
we could feel it wasn’t a fraud  
*Please Let Me Repay You*  
*Please Let Me Repay You*  
*Please Let Me Repay You*  
by following your words  
practicing what we’ve heard  
some people think that i’ve just gone mad  
others will say that it’s too darn bad  
long white robes and a shaven head  
i only do what Kṛṣṇa says  
i was first initiated in ’74  
i promised to my *guru* i would sin no more  
no meat, fish, eggs, no illicit sex  
if you live your life right, you’re guaranteed success  
i go out when i can just to preach the truth  
an eye for an eye, and a tooth for tooth  
ain’t nobody out there who can change my mind  
’cause i’ve seen it all before and i know their kind  
if you see me coming, better chant the name  
tell me about **Kṛṣṇa**, don’t play your games  
i’ve got my beads in my hand and a book in my arms  
if the *mantra* don’t get you, try the *Bhāgavatam*  
i chant ***16 Rounds***  
so what do you get?  
another day older and closer to death  
Yamarāja can’t you see i’m a man of God  
i owe my soul to Śrīla Prabhupāda

*Your servants in New Dvārakā dhāma, Los Angeles, California, USA.*  
(written by Nirantara dāsa)

## New Gokula

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We are not in a position to know directly what Kṛṣṇa wants, but we do know what you want, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You have given us the infrastructure of an organization (ISKCON) that, if based on love and trust and following your instructions, will be successful in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness in a manner that is pleasing to you. Our success lies in pleasing you because pleasing you means pleasing Kṛṣṇa, the reservoir of all pleasure. This is a very simple concept, yet we have the tendency to complicate it with our desire to lord it over. Is that not the reason we find ourselves in this material world in the first place?

When your lotus feet first touched this planet, it was destined to be transformed. The spiritual revolution began in America the moment you set foot off the *Jaladuta*. For us you are the control point of the spiritual wave that is ever expanding over the globe. We are and will always be indebted to you. Just like the sun, which illuminates the entire horizon by its presence, your short stay here with us gave light and life to those fortunate souls who just happened to be in the right place at the right time. If we could possess even an ounce of your transcendental nature and present it “as it is” to the rest of the world, we would be justified in saying “we are your disciples.” If we could only follow in your footsteps and not meander off, then those footsteps would lead us to you, and that is where we want to be, in the shade of your lotus feet. Please allow us eternal shelter at your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

*Your servants in New Gokula, Vancouver, Canada.*

## New Goloka

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

O Vaiṣṇavas near and far, come.  
O gentle devotees, please come.  
Come, let us gather.  
From all the world, gather.  
To make of ourselves a great congregation,  
Forgetful of status and all limitation.  
In love, let us raise a great invocation  
And turn all our minds in sweet meditation  
On his lotus feet now, please come.  
To praise Śrīla Prabhupāda, come.  
So not to make fault before we begin,  
First consider our place in relation to him,  
Whose glory is spread throughout the three worlds  
By bright perfect beings who may broadcast the pearls  
Of his teachings and mercy to realms we can’t know.  
Where spiritual masters and purified souls  
Bow down to his feet, recognize him in ways  
Not revealed yet to us in these earthbound days.  
Emphatically thus! Prabhupāda is not made  
Any more glorious by our meager praise.  
Not to say, though, the prayers of the small are in vain.  
For does not the Lord of all extend each the same  
Great birthright of His loving service bestowed  
On the purest and wisest and highest of souls?  
But what of us, long out of practice of love,  
Who had soiled ourselves sadly in lustful pursuit of  
The phantom attractions of this pale place,  
Who had willfully, skillfully sought the embrace  
Of Māyā in darkness, illusion, disgrace?  
O how could we hope to ever escape—  
To stand in His grace—again see His face?  
Then causelessly, mercifully the Almighty Lord  
Dispatched His dear servant to Earth to afford  
Us miserable moribund souls the rare chance  
To serve His beloved, and thereby advance.  
O Wonder! O Joy! The impossible solved—  
Śrīla Prabhupāda came to deliver us all.  
As Guru, Founder-*ācārya,* and Friend  
He decisively, expertly brought to an end  
The embarrassing ignorance of self and false pride  
That polluted our hearts and clouded our eyes.  
“This world is all Kṛṣṇa’s and thus should be used  
For His loving service, instead of refused.”  
Then Prabhupāda taught how the Lord could be served,  
The transcendent etiquette to be observed.  
“Don’t try to serve Kṛṣṇa directly,” he said.  
“*Dāsa-dāsa-anudāsa* instead.  
“You must serve Him together instead of alone;  
In this way your love for me will be shown.  
To keep you together in love, lest you roam,  
I give you this ISKCON, your Shelter, your Home.”  
Thus Prabhupāda’s victory of love was complete,  
And all sought attachment to his lotus feet—  
Miraculous ambition for souls once near dead.  
This perfection fulfills all desires, it is said.  
What shall we desire, then, for what should we pray?  
Surely no selfish wish—that might lead us astray.  
Something for Prabhupāda! “Of course!” we proclaim.  
To please and serve him is our heart’s only aim.  
And what would he like from us? No need to guess:  
Śrīla Prabhupāda has just one humble request.  
So *Saṅkīrtan! Saṅkīrtan! Saṅkīrtan!* go  
Throughout the whole world, far and wide, high and low.  
*Saṅkīrtan! Saṅkīrtan! Saṅkīrtan!* may  
The sweet holy names expand every way.  
May the Golden Lord please make more chanters until  
Every street, every town, every city is filled,  
And only the Names are heard anymore,  
Drowning the world in a great rising roar  
Of “Rādhā-Golokānanda *kī jaya*! Nitāi-Gaura!  
*Haribol! Haribol!* Nitāi-Gaura *haribol*!  
Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa  
Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare  
Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma  
Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare”  
O loving devotees, may this vision remain  
As our most cherished goal, our life’s only aim.  
To help Śrīla Prabhupāda in each our small way,  
To bring the whole world back to Godhead to stay.

Humbly praying to be allowed entrance into that great ecstatic congregation of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s followers,

*The devotees of New Goloka dhāma, Hillsborough, North Carolina, USA.*  
(written by Baṭu Gopāla dāsa)

## New Govardhana (Australia)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**For Tiny Jīvas Like Us**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

It was for tiny *jīvas* like us that the order of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī had you walk among us with great compassion, giving knowledge to the ignorant and shedding light in the darkness. We absorbed that knowledge and bathed in that light, but now, with the unavoidable consequences of your disappearance, we’re experiencing a different facet of your mercy, for which we were not at all prepared. It is called separation, and it burns our heart like fire. Today, on your Vyāsa-pūjā, it is especially severe, so now we are bowing down at your lotus feet, remembering Your Divine Grace and getting some relief. We can now offer these words of truth in your glorification.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is indeed our great fortune that simply by our choosing to serve you, the uneclipsed condition of our soul is gradually awakened. There is no doubt about it. The pleasure of the Lord is realized by senses that have become purified by accepting your guidance. However, it is also a great misfortune when still we tend to misuse our free will and disobey your instructions. And when still the witch of Māyā immediately eclipses and captures us. In that foolish condition we cannot even understand the justice of our own suffering. We become expert at laying blame on others, even upon God and, dare I say it, sometimes even upon his devotees. But the simple truth of the matter is this: when we fall down, it is of our own free will, and there is no other reason.

For tiny *jīvas* like us, there will always exist a fine line separating truth from illusion. We exist on that line and are therefore called *taṭa-stha,* “marginal,” and in practical terms, whether we dwell in truth or in illusion depends simply on whether we obey or disobey the Lord. On behalf of the Lord, you mercifully revealed the existence of that line, and you kindly offered us, again, the choice of our freedom.

Since then, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have deemed obedience as supremely important. Obedience is our initial, middle, and final lesson. It is the fundamental necessity for any progress on the path of our Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Without learning obedience, tiny *jīvas* like us are destined to remain neophyte, unable to make any progress beyond speculative imagination and mere sentimentality.

Therefore, our simple prayer on this day is that we may continuously and conscientiously practice obedience in the service of your lotus feet, and that we always tread this sublime and glorious path, which we know pleases the Lord. Śrīla Prabhupāda, somehow or other please keep us mindful of our need for obedience, for without obedience to the spiritual master—in other words, without obedience to you—tiny *jīvas* like us cannot even see the path, what to speak of traverse it.

We beg to remain,

*Your obedient followers in ISKCON’s New Govardhana Community, Australia.*  
(written by Veṇugopāla dāsa)

## New Govardhana (San Diego)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances to His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at his lotus feet.”**

**“Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this blessed day of your divine appearance celebration, all of us from the New Govardhana community beg to offer you words of both praise and gratitude. Your divine appearance and activities have set a sweet and eternal life course for all of us who have been touched by your kind and ever-fresh influence. This is a day to honor you as the wonderful founder and bona fide *ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness, a representative of Vyāsadeva and indeed of the whole Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava *sampradāya*. You are both the *ācārya* and the kind father and grandfather for all of us. This day is also a day when we feel and express increased thankfulness to Your Divine Grace as our eternal spiritual master, remembering how you were so kind to take up the mission of your spiritual master and come to the West to deliver all of us. We are eternally indebted and thankful to you, and we pray to be eternally connected with the dust of your sweet lotus feet.

In honor of Your Divine Grace, we would like to cite one of your conversations, in which you emphasize how all of us, as your followers and as Vaiṣṇavas, need to cooperate and treat one another with love and respect while serving together in your mission. As members of your ISKCON and the Vaiṣṇava community, we recall how Your Divine Grace emphasized that we should all cooperate in serving you, and how much that would please you. As a father is very pleased when his family members get along harmoniously, so you are pleased when we work together in that way. Since the basis of success in *bhakti* is a sincere and genuine heart guided by the spiritual master, we pray that we may have this kind of heart to receive your messages submissively and work together to serve you.

The setting of the conversation with Your Divine Grace is Vṛndāvana, November 24, 1976. This conversation follows a discussion you just had with some disciples on how ISKCON managers should work with the devotees under them. The essence here is not only the practical direction that the devotees should be trained, but also how our hearts should be in our dealings with fellow Vaiṣṇavas. In the excerpts from your instructions cited below, it is very relevant how you emphasize the mood or mindset that all of us as Vaiṣṇavas must have in our dealings with one another as we cooperate to serve your mission.

**Devotee:** Actually, we’ve been speaking the last few days and these problems can be solved.

**Śrīla Prabhupāda:**No, it is Kṛṣṇa’s service. Everyone is offering voluntary service. So not that anybody’s paid....This bureaucracy is not.... Train him. Train him. If he does not know, train him. But things must be done very nicely by cooperation. That is wanted. Everyone should remember that we are serving Kṛṣṇa, and everyone should remember, “The other person is serving Kṛṣṇa. And because he is serving Kṛṣṇa, he is not my servant, he is my master.” That should be always in view. Therefore we address “*Prabhu,*” “You are my master.” We never address, “You are my servant.”...*Prabhu* means “master.” One should always think that everyone is his master because he’s serving the Master. This is our philosophy....

...You should always remember that you are helping me. Without your help I cannot do anything, so you do not be disturbed. Try to help me. That is my request. Thank you very much.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray that by your mercy all of us may enter deeply into this mood of mutual respect, love, and cooperation in serving your mission. Please let this and all of your instructions become one with our hearts. All of us at New Govardhana thank you for this opportunity to glorify Your Divine Grace and for your giving us the foundation for this wonderful community and the association here of so many wonderful *Prabhus*!

*Your humble servants at New Govardhana dhāma, San Diego, California, USA.*

## New Guṇḍicā

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

How often do we recite your *praṇāma* mantra, and how infrequently meditate on its meaning? How many times do we repeat the words *pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe,* and how little understand their import? Almost thirty-eight years after your arrival in the West, we still do not fully comprehend the immensity of your sacrifice in leaving the shelter of the holy *dhāma,* the compass of your causeless mercy in bringing Kṛṣṇa consciousness to our shores, or the momentousness of your unprecedented mission.

We offer our prostrated obeisances unto you time after time, addressing you as Viṣṇupāda, “one who is ever at the feet of Lord Viṣṇu,” and *kṛṣṇa-preṣṭha,* “one who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa,” but do we actually realize that indeed you are always in the spiritual world at the feet of the Lord, because you are His eternal associate? Are we ever fully aware that it is of such a one as you that Kṛṣṇa says *na tathā me priyatama ātma-yonir na śaṅkaraḥ/ na ca saṅkarṣaṇo na śrīr naivātmā ca yathā bhavān*? (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 11.14.15) Neither Brahmā, Śiva, Saṅkarṣaṇa, Lakṣmī, *nor even His own Self* is as dear to Kṛṣṇa as you are, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

And why does Kṛṣṇa love you more than His own Self? Because you are the steadfast servant of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura (*namas te sārasvate deve*) and your only concern is to preach the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu (*gaura-vāṇī pracāriṇe*). That is your perfection: absolute dedication to the fulfillment of the desire of Śrīla Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, without the slightest deviation from the pristine path delineated by the *mahājanas*. In his commentary on *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 11.14.16, Śrīdhara Svāmī states that Kṛṣṇa follows a pure devotee such as you to get the dust of your lotus feet, so that He may thus purify His entire creation (*pūyeya mad-antarvati-brahmāṇḍani pavitrī-kuryām iti bhavenety arthaḥ*).

You left the sacrosanct shelter of Vṛndāvana, where your beloved Kṛṣṇacandra eternally manifests His sweetest pastimes, for these lands, steeped in the bitterness of voidism and impersonalism (*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi*). You had unflinching faith in the order of Śrī Guru, and unalloyed love for Yaśodānandana Kṛṣṇa and His holy name. You came with a few books, a bag of dry cereal, and forty rupees, to deliver the West (*pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*). And it was more than enough.

Many of us labor under the immature conception that your unparalleled achievements unfolded after your blessed arrival in Boston aboard the *Jaladuta*. For almost forty years we have feebly attempted to praise your prodigious writing, your indefatigable travel around the world, and your relentless preaching in the face of all adversity, as if these acts, exceptional and matchless as they are, were the measure of your worth, or the cause of our deliverance.

We fail to realize that you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the beloved of Kṛṣṇa, the pure devotee, need not exert yourself to accomplish your aims. The Lord accomplishes for you whatever there is to be achieved. Kṛṣṇa allows His devotee to manifest a pastime of struggle in order to glorify him, but not because it is necessary. In your purport to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 3.9.39, you have stated in pellucid terms:

A pure devotee of the Lord like Brahmā and those in his line of disciplic succession always desire that the Lord be known all over the universe by each and every one of the living entities. That desire of the devotee is always blessed by the Lord.

In our ignorance, we wonder how, in a scant twelve years, you were able to fulfill the prophecy of Śrī Caitanya that His name would be chanted in every town and village of the world. We forget that the Lord has given His word

*ya etena pumān nityaṁ stutvā stotreṇa māṁ bhajet*  
*tasyāśu samprasīdeyaṁ sarva-kāma-vareśvaraḥ*

**“Any human being who prays like Brahmā, and who thus worships Me, shall very soon be blessed with the fulfillment of all his desires, for I am the Lord of all benediction.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **3.9.40)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, after a thirty-five-day sea journey from Calcutta, you reached Boston’s Commonwealth Pier at 5:30 a.m. on September 17, 1965, bringing with you the first rays of the blazing sun of Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement. You took a short walk into our city and then returned to the ship. As you awaited the ship’s departure, you composed a prayer to your beloved Kṛṣṇa. You prayed like Brahmā:

*tomāra icchāya saba hoy māyā-baśa*  
*tomāra icchāya nāśa māyār parāśa*  
*taba icchā hoyjadi tādera uddhār*  
*bujhibe niścai tabe kathā se tomār*

**“All living entities have come under the control of the illusory energy by Your will, and therefore, if You like, by Your will they can also be released from the clutches of illusion. I wish that You may deliver them. Therefore if You so desire their deliverance, then only will they be able to understand Your message.” (*****Mārkine Bhāgavata-dharma*****, 6–7).**

With that prayer, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you accomplished our deliverance. The manifestation of that hallowed deed is unfolding minute by minute, hour by hour, day by day. The pure desire of the pure devotee accomplished this deliverance of the West, which is progressively becoming a manifest reality by your mercy.

You acknowledged as much to Mr. Ruben a few months later, in New York City. You met him on a park bench, and you told this friendly subway conductor, “I am not a poor man, I am rich. There are temples and books, they are existing, they are there, but the time is separating us from them.” You knew then, as you always do, the heart of Kṛṣṇa. You knew that He had heard your prayer and granted your desire.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this day when we commemorate your sacred advent upon this earth, we pray that you, who entreated the Lord on our behalf, will grant this earnest desire of our hearts: that we may serve you and sing your glories, wherever we might find ourselves, life after life.

*Your grateful disciples, grand-disciples, and followers in New Guṇḍicā, Boston, Massachusetts, USA.*

## New Jagannātha Purī

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

“The spiritual master is to be honored as much as the Supreme Lord, because he is the most confidential servitor of the Lord. This is acknowledged in all revealed scriptures and followed by all authorities. Therefore I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master, who is a bona fide representative of Śrī Hari \[Kṛṣṇa\].”

“My only wish is to have my consciousness purified by the words emanating from his lotus mouth. Attachment to his lotus feet is the perfection that fulfills all desires.”

By your grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have been converted from an animalistic existence to aspiring devotees of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. You have saved us from the greatest danger—wasting our valuable human form of life and transmigrating to the lower species. You have asked us to take what you have given us and in turn give it to everyone that we meet. In 1972 you wrote a letter to Trai dāsa, then the Berkeley temple president, in which you stated:

That \[Berkeley\] is one of our most important branches especially because it is located just in a very large university area, well known in your country as a famous seat of learning. So now it will be very important to flood Berkeley city with our books and literatures, because if we can convince the intelligent class of men of our Krishna philosophy then our success for changing the position of your country and the rest of the world from a very dangerous condition is assured.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have saved us from this dangerous condition. Please give us your blessings so that we may help you in your mission to continue to save others from the same dangers. Please help us in our preaching efforts at this famous seat of learning, the University of California at Berkeley.

We know that you are always here with us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and that you are always taking care of us. As you wrote to “My dear Locana das, Nalinikanta, and all the boys and girls at Berkeley Center”:

I am always thinking of that beautiful, quiet neighborhood in Berkeley, and of all my wonderful disciples there, so when I return to USA I shall certainly come there for some time....At least I will be present there with you all in spirit, *as always*, you may know that for certain.” \[Italics ours\]

Your Divine Grace, we hear the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* from you every morning. We take notes on your lectures and discuss your main points. In this way we try to understand and remember all the nectarean instructions you have given us. We meditate upon your words.

“My only wish is to have my consciousness purified by the words emanating from his lotus mouth.” Please give us the benediction that our understanding of Kṛṣṇa consciousness will increase and our attachment to your lotus feet and Kṛṣṇa will grow tighter and tighter. Attachment to your lotus feet is the perfection that fulfills all our desires.

*Your servants at New Jagannātha Purī dhāma, Berkeley, California, USA.*

## New Kāmyavan

Respected Grandfather Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We, the devotees in New Kāmyavan, offer our respectful obeisances unto your lotus feet.

You are Lord Kṛṣṇa’s most beloved. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanya and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism. You are on the topmost platform of *sannyāsa*. You began ISKCON personally and spread the glories of Lord Hari all over the world. All your life you have been chanting Lord Kṛṣṇa’s holy name and dancing. You are just like an ocean of love for the Lord, and everything you have done is for the benefit of everyone in the world. You have received Lord Caitanya’s full blessings, and you took up the mission of delivering all the conditioned souls in this Age of Kali.

You are expert in studying all the revealed Vedic scriptures, and you yourself composed more than sixty transcendental works. You are an ocean of mercy, the friend of the poor, and the lord and master of the devotees. You open the eyes of the fallen souls with transcendental knowledge. The words emanating from your lotus mouth purify the hearts of the fallen souls. By your grace, the embodied souls can cross the ocean of material suffering and obtain the mercy of Kṛṣṇa.

By your mercy, your excellent disciples have overcome many difficulties and have fearlessly come to this atheistic place to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness and deliver the fallen souls. We are greatly fortunate to be able to read your transcendental writings and to obtain the message of God—Kṛṣṇa consciousness—by disciplic succession.

Now, in this small and remote place, we have developed new devotees. Some of us have already had the good fortune of taking shelter of your disciples. Many more have received your transcendental books and heard the glories of Hare Kṛṣṇa. The people here are gradually waking up and reviving their original Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Everything that has happened depends on your mercy.

On this auspicious anniversary of your appearance, we sincerely pray for your blessings in order to glorify your endless devotional qualities and spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness further and deeper.

*All the devotees in New Kāmyavan, Khasadeśa.*

## New Māyāpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and respectful obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace, all glories to your 2003 Vyāsa-pūjā celebration!

We are born in the darkest regions of ignorance, but by the torchlight of the knowledge of Kṛṣṇa consciousness you have opened our eyes. Thus we all bow down to offer our most humble and respectful obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

It is not easy to go on without your physical presence. But to appreciate more the nectar of your unique example and wonderful message, we have to realize just for a moment what life would be like if you had not come to us in this *nirviśeṣa* and *śūnyavādi* world! And this could have been the case if you had not had such firm faith in the instructions of your spiritual master and Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu! So even if today we may have difficulties in practicing Kṛṣṇa consciousness and in spreading your extraordinary movement, we have to remember every day how you took so many risks to come save us and how you were always enthusiastic, determined, and patient in fulfilling the instructions of your Guru Mahārāja.

So now it is our duty to let everyone know the very special nature of your appearance in this world. You gave everything in terms of the knowledge and practice of *bhakti,* and also for the conduct of day-to-day life. For this reason and so many others you are our eternal well-wisher. We still wonder—and the historians will wonder even more—how you could spread your ISKCON movement so quickly in this age of hypocrisy and quarrel. You brought the topmost philosophy of life to the most materialistic and atheistic places of the world. You provided wonderfully high thinking and strong speaking for this world of speculation and pride. You gave the best of everything because you always wanted to satisfy the supreme friend of everyone. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your infinite and most powerful compassion. Actually, it is not possible to fully describe your wonderful glories because they are unlimited. So please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, give us all the capacity to please you. Only then will our lives become successful!

His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*! Śrīla Prabhupāda’s Vyāsa-pūjā *kī jaya*!

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for taking the time to read this offering.

*Your eternally indebted servants in New Māyāpur dhāma, France.*  
(written by Madhvācārya dāsa)

## New Nandagram

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories to Śrī Śrī Guru-Gaurāṅga!

In February 1936, in Bombay, India, the members of the Gauḍīya Maṭha listened intently to your powerful and eloquent words in honor of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. At that time you stated, “We adore this auspicious day as Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā-tithi because the *ācārya* is the living representative of Vyāsadeva, the divine compiler of the *Vedas,* the *Purāṇas,* the *Bhagavad-gītā,* the *Mahābhārata,* and the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*.” (*The Science of Self-Realization,* Chapter 2). Similarly, we also adore this auspicious day when we relish your incomparable achievements in distributing the literature of Śrīla Vyāsadeva all over the world.

Because you actually represent Śrīla Vyāsadeva, you did not change anything that you heard from your spiritual master and the previous *ācāryas*. This you stated on a number of occasions, such as in a letter to Jadurāṇī,

Thank you for appreciating my humble efforts on behalf of my spiritual master, Bhaktisiddhanta Sarasvati Gosvami Maharaja, and Lord Caitanya. Whatever I have done it is due to their potency in Krishna Consciousness. My only achievement is that I have not changed what they have taught. \[Letter, 6 July 1973\]

And similarly in a letter to Bhīma dāsa:

I have not done anything personally, very wonderful. I am simply serving my spiritual master, Srila Bhaktisiddhanta Sarasvati Goswami Maharaja, and all the acaryas in the disciplic succession. If I have done anything of credit it is that I have not changed their teachings. I have not added anything of my own interpretation. \[Letter, 16 May 1974

In your deep humility you refrained from stating the extent of your glories. Not only did you present the teachings of Vyāsadeva unchanged, but you specifically presented the most confidential teachings of the *Vedas,* i.e., the science of *bhakti-yoga,* by which one can achieve the highest goal of life—pure love for Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. In order to teach this supreme transcendental science one must be a pure devotee of the Lord. This is your glory—that you are a first-class pure devotee of the Lord, whose only goal is to serve the Lord by delivering the message of Godhead to the conditioned souls in this material world. Being a pure devotee of the Lord, you are fully empowered by the Lord to act as His representative. Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s and Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s empowering His pure devotees to deliver the conditioned souls is mentioned in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Antya* 2.4):

The Lord delivered the fallen souls in some places by meeting them directly, in other places by empowering a pure devotee, and in still other places by appearing before someone Himself.

In the purport to *Caitanya-caritāmṛta*, *Ādi* 8.39, you have explained that only the pure devotees of the Lord can properly represent Śrīla Vyāsadeva by teaching the ultimate conclusions of the *Vedas,*

Transcendental literature that strictly follows the Vedic principles and the conclusion of the *Purāṇas* and *pāñcarātrika-vidhi* can be written only by a pure devotee. It is not possible for a common man to write books on *bhakti,* for his writings will not be effective. He may be a very great scholar and may be expert in presenting literature in flowery language, but this is not at all helpful in understanding transcendental literature. Even if transcendental literature is written in faulty language, it is acceptable if it is written by a devotee, whereas so-called transcendental literature written by a mundane scholar, even if it is a very highly polished literary presentation, cannot be accepted. The secret in a devotee’s writing is that when he writes about the pastimes of the Lord, the Lord helps him; he does not write alone. As stated in the *Bhagavad-gītā* (10.10), *dadāmi buddhi-yogaṁ taṁ yena mām upayānti te*. Since a devotee writes in service to the Lord, the Lord from within gives him so much intelligence that he sits down near the Lord and goes on writing books.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your teachings are very wonderful and not easily understood or followed by a conditioned soul such as ourselves. However, you have taught us that we will eventually be purified if we continue to hear your words and study your books. In a discussion with Hayagrīva and Kīrtanānanda in Boston on December 24, 1969, you stated,

When Guru Mahārāja was speaking, at least my brain was puzzled. (*laughter*) Even he would speak in Bengali, it was very difficult to understand. He was speaking from a very, very high platform. But I wanted to hear him. That’s all. Even I did not understand it. That he appreciated, (*laughs*) that “This boy does not go away. He hears.” Actually that was my position. In the beginning I could not understand what he was speaking, but I wanted to hear him. That’s all. I was very much anxious to hear him. That he marked. And he was kindly pleased on me, that “He wants to hear. He does not go away.” That was my policy, that “let me hear. Even I do not understand, let me hear.” That’s all. Yes. Actually, I did not understand in the beginning what he was speaking. So Bhaktisiddhānta’s writing is not very easy to understand. Yes. But we should try, read and read again, and simply that vibration will help us. That’s all. It is transcendental vibration, not that everyone will understand. But if you simply give aural reception to the vibration, that will make him advanced, not exactly that anyone has to understand it. Yes. Just like a man is sleeping and somebody is calling him. In his sleeping condition he does not understand. By calling, calling, calling, he gets up because that vibration is there. Not that in his sleeping condition he is understanding what is this sound coming. So similarly, we should give reception to the transcendental vibrations made by Kṛṣṇa and His bona fide representatives. That will make us awakened. Not that we understand everything.

On this most auspicious day we pray at your lotus feet that we can always have the benediction of studying your teachings and remembering your words. By this method we can remain inspired to serve the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa and gradually make progress in transcendental realization. You have confirmed this in your concluding words to the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta,*

Although according to material vision His Divine Grace *Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura Prabhupāda* passed away from this material world on the last day of December, 1936, I still consider His Divine Grace to be always present with me by his *vāṇī,* his words. There are two ways of association—by *vāṇī* and by *vapu*. *Vāṇī* means words, and *vapu* means physical presence. Physical presence is sometimes appreciable and sometimes not, but *vāṇī* continues to exist eternally. Therefore we must take advantage of the *vāṇī,* not the physical presence. The *Bhagavad-gītā,* for example, is the *vāṇī* of Lord Kṛṣṇa. Although Kṛṣṇa was personally present five thousand years ago and is no longer physically present from the materialistic point of view, the *Bhagavad-gītā* continues....

I think that His Divine Grace Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura is always seeing my activities and guiding me within my heart by his words. As it is said in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, tene brahma hṛdā ya ādi-kavaye*. Spiritual inspiration comes from within the heart, wherein the Supreme Personality of Godhead, in His Paramātmā feature, is always sitting with all His devotees and associates.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you taught us that along with the process of hearing from the bona fide guru we should also render practical service to Śrī Gurudeva in order to accelerate the benefits of hearing transcendental knowledge. This you explained in your glorification of your spiritual master in 1936 in Bombay,

It is accordingly said in *Śrī Gītā: tad viddhi praṇipātena paripraśnena sevayā/ upadekṣyanti te jñānaṁ jñāninas tattva-darśinaḥ*. “Just approach the wise and bona fide spiritual master. Surrender unto him first and try to understand him by inquiries and service. Such a wise spiritual master will enlighten you with transcendental knowledge, for he has already known the Absolute Truth.” To receive the transcendental knowledge we must completely surrender ourselves to the real *ācārya* in a spirit of ardent inquiry and service. Actual performance of service to the Absolute under the guidance of the *ācārya* is the only vehicle by which we can assimilate the transcendental knowledge. Today’s meeting for offering our humble services and homage to the feet of the Ācāryadeva will enable us to be favored with the capacity of assimilating the transcendental knowledge so kindly transmitted by him to all persons without distinction.

Therefore, on this auspicious day of Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā, we humbly pray for your blessings that we may always be able to hear your teachings, even though we may not fully understand them, and that we may have the opportunity to always render service to your lotus feet, even though we are not qualified to do so.

*Your servants at the New Nandagram farm community, Victoria, Australia.*  
(written by Gaṅgeśvara dāsa)

## New Nilācala

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humbles obeisances under the dust of your sacred lotus feet, which are the shelter of all the fallen souls like us.

You gave us a perfect philosophy and taught us that our duty is to present Kṛṣṇa consciousness in an appropriate way and follow the rules and regulations. In that way, you explained, we would purify our lives and get the potency to convince all people about the goal of life—pure love for Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla prabhupāda, we beg you to make us your instruments for distributing your sacred books, preaching the glories of the holy name, opening new temples, and distributing *prasādam*. We know that these are very difficult tasks for us, but if you empower us with your unlimited mercy, then we can succeed and help expand your most important mission.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are a *karuṇā-sindhu,* an ocean of mercy. We request that you please never withdraw your mercy from us, your devotees in New Nilācala, because without your mercy we cannot fight against powerful Māyā, who is always offering us sense gratification.

*Your insignificants and most fallen aspiring servants in New Nilācala mandira, Guadalajara, Mexico.*  
(written by Premānanda dāsa)

## New Orleans

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace, who are very dear to Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Rādhā-Kānta.

Here in Saṅkīrtana Purī Dhāma we hold a two-day festival in honor of your Vyāsa-pūjā day. As you know, most of the devotees here are your grand-disciples. But we always try to understand your mercy by respecting your disciples and appreciating their sincere service for your mission. Therefore we respect and offer our gratitude to all our uncles as much we do to our fathers, our initiating gurus. That is why we hold a special tow-day program on your Vyāsa-pūjā day—so that all the disciples of Your Dive Grace can be honored and given time to speak of your various pastimes. We feel this festival is important in helping us remain loyal to Your Divine Grace and dedicated to our disciplic succession.

Preaching from your Saṅkīrtana Purī to the people in New Orleans is a very important and difficult task. We have completed major renovation work on the temple in 2003, but we still have to paint this beautiful temple, and that is why we held another fund-raising campaign on Janmāṣṭamī. We printed Janmāṣṭamī souvenir books, as we did last year, to help raise funds and raise our congregation’s awareness of how important it is to properly care for Kṛṣṇa’s temple. Our *gṛhastha* house had not been in ISKCON’s name from the time it was bought in 1980. After struggling hard for a bank loan, we have been able to get the property in your Society’s name. We are sure you are pleased with the devotees who struggled and succeeded in securing the house. It is time to renovate this house as well, and to have a sun-house for our Tulasī plants. We think all this will be also possible by your mercy.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our eternal savior and the source of all our inspiration.
