Regardless of our shortcomings, we are preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness in this part of the world because we have accepted your transcendental motto: “Preaching is the essence. Books are the basis. Utility is the principle. And purity is the force.” With great effort, over the last year we have been able to increase the participants in our Sunday feast program, upgraded the care of the Deities, and increased book distribution. We are realizing more and more that if we can maintain good *sādhana,* as you have taught, we should be able to have purity, and only then will our preaching be successful.

On this very auspicious anniversary of your appearance, we pray from our hearts for your blessings so that we can remain sincere in our service and continue preaching the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness you gave us. That is the only way to appreciate your hard struggle to fulfill Lord Caitanya’s mission.

*Your humble servants in New Orleans, Louisiana, USA.*

## New Pānihāṭi

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and prostrated obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

With the passing of each year we realize more and more the incredible mercy you have bestowed upon us in the form of your ISKCON movement. Within this movement you have installed many potent and powerful processes that, when followed properly, can produce within us the various levels of Kṛṣṇa consciousness you so masterfully describe in your books.

The foremost process is the chanting of the Lord’s holy names. The importance of chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa may sometimes appear to be eclipsed by the seemingly more pressing duties related to spreading this vital movement. But in reality it all works together in concert. In fact, one cannot function without the other. How can we spread the movement of chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa if we don’t chant Hare Kṛṣṇa? How can we remain focused if we don’t chant Hare Kṛṣṇa? How can we expect to achieve freedom from Māyā’s influence if we don’t chant Hare Kṛṣṇa?

Now, some might say that I am “preaching to the choir,” but I would respond by saying that members of a choir sometimes forget their parts in a song or recital.

Your Vyāsa-pūjā celebration is an appropriate time for us to focus on those things that were most important to you. These are the things you placed your stamp of approval on and in relation to which you left us awesome mandates for us to mold our lives around. There are certainly many things that we know you were clear about and that we can place our complete faith in. The first of these is chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa. Then, of course, the distribution of your books. Next, Deity worship, which is so vitally important for consistent development of the proper feelings for the Lord in His various forms. And who could argue about the importance of such festivals as Ratha-yātrā, Janmāṣṭamī, Gaura Pūrṇimā, Vyāsa-pūjā, and Lord Nṛsimhadeva’s appearance? And the list goes on. It is practically unthinkable that we could for a moment be confused about how to carry on the work of this movement, when everything is so clear and simple.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you worked so hard to establish this movement, and it is most appropriate that we, as your followers, continue in that tradition of hard work for Lord Kṛṣṇa. But if we allow ourselves to become at all confused about the process, then it will be practically impossible to achieve the type of focused activity required to push forward this wonderful and important movement. Therefore we pray to you on this blessed Vyāsa-pūjā day that you kindly watch over us as your sons, daughters, grandsons, and granddaughters so that we do not stray from the clear and precise process you have given us. And we also pray to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa that we may be empowered to please you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, by all our individual and combined efforts in the matter of spreading the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement around the world.

*Your humble servants at New Pānihāṭi dhāma, ISKCON’s temple in Atlanta, Georgia, USA.*

## New Ramaṇ Reti

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine soothing lotus feet. All glories, all glories, all glories unto Your Divine Grace!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you appeared on this planet in 1896 to fulfill the prophecy of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanyadeva: “In every town and village My name will be spread.”

This material world consists of the Paramātmā (the Lord), the *jīvātmas* (souls), *prakṛti* (material nature), *karma* (fruitive activities), and *kāla* (time). But in the spiritual world there is Bhagavān (the Supreme Personality of Godhead), the *bhakta* (devotee), *bhakti,* (devotional service), eternally present time, and *svarūpa-śakti* (spiritual nature). As an *ācārya,* you showed us how to go from the material world to the spiritual world by following the path of *bhakti.* In so doing you introduced us to Bhagavan, the Supreme Personality of Godhead Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and established this ISKCON society, which is needed for creating a unified endeavor to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Kali-yuga.

While sitting on a park bench in New York in 1965, you stated that you had so many temples and so many devotees. At that time you did not visibly have anything, but you had perfect vision. On July 29, 1971, when you visited Gainesville, Florida, you remarked, “It is so nice to see so many young boys and girls here in this remote corner of the world, so far away from the birthplace of Lord Caitanya.”

Well, fifteen miles away from Gainesville, in an even more remote corner of the world, is your New Ramaṇ Reti farm community. Twelve years ago Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurasundara were being worshiped in a mobile trailer by the few devotee families residing here. Now more than four hundred of your sons, daughters, grandchildren, and, yes, great-grandchildren are residing here and serving you. By your mercy and blessings, we have built a large, beautiful new temple where we daily worship Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurasundara, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara, and Lord Nṛsiṁha-deva. We come together to honor and celebrate the Vaiṣṇava festivals and holy days you gave us.

In 1971, in a letter to the then temple president, you requested, “Do something wonderful there in Gainesville, Florida.” Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are now in the third phase of building up the community. We are planning to build a *prasādam* hall and a reception room with offices and guest rooms, and to expand the kitchen and the *pūjārī* room. Visiting devotees comment that this community can become like the spiritual world, with thousands of people can visiting and experiencing spiritual bliss. By your mercy and blessings, and by our following your instructions, this can become possible gradually, step by step. As you said, “Impossible is a word in a fool’s dictionary.” We are fools, and our vision is defective, but having so many devotees in this community gives us a responsibility to set an example for one another, as well as for guests, by properly following the path you have laid down for us.

In a letter to one of your disciples in 1972, you wrote,

I can only suggest...but I do not force anyone. After all, you are working so hard to please Krsna only out of love for me, so there can be no question of force if love is there. We should not ever try to force anyone or reduce our Society to an impersonal business exchange. This will kill everything. Our only purpose in every endeavor is simply to make advancement in spiritual life or in pleasing Krsna.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please pray to your beloved Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa to instill within us that love toward Your Divine Grace and each other, so that by loving each other we can unite together as a whole community and carry out the preaching responsibilities you have given us.

All glories to Your Divine Grace!

*Your eternal servants at New Ramaṇ Reti dhāma, the ISKCON rural community in Alachua, Florida, USA.*  
(written by Bhadra dāsa)

## New Remuṇā

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you, who have delivered us from a life of inane and futile material existence. By your causeless mercy you have given us joy, hope, knowledge, and unparalleled meaning in our lives. You continue to empower us with enthusiasm and courage through your books, through the beautiful *darśana* of our Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Kṣīracorā-Gopīnātha, and the priceless association and inspiration of all your devotees. We want to sincerely and humbly thank you for your continuing love and compassion.

Since your last Vyāsa-pūjā we have had much to celebrate at New Remuṇā *dhāma*. It has been thirty years since the installation of our Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Kṣīracorā-Gopīnātha. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you personally arranged for Their Lordships to come to Toronto. When informed that our newly-arrived Kṛṣṇa had an ample tummy, you laughed and said, “That is because your Kṛṣṇa is Kṣīracorā-Gopīnātha!” To this day we have offered twelve pots of *kṣīra* every evening. To commemorate this wonderful event, former Toronto devotees from all over North America gathered for a two-day reunion. We chanted, danced, reminisced, relished fabulous *prasādam,* and wowed Toronto with the biggest *harināma* in years. Your presence was tangible at our reunion. We thank you again and again for arranging for Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Kṣīracorā-Gopīnātha to grace us with Their merciful presence for so many years.

In addition, our devotees are planning a substantial and very necessary renovation of the temple. Architects are helping us conceptualize the future of ISKCON in Toronto, committees are coming together, and plans are being made. Central to this, of course, is your spirit for preaching and your enthusiasm for developing Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Toronto. You told us in 1975 that you were personally praying to Kṛṣṇa that we would be able to purchase this church. You personally toured the building and saw the future clearly in your mind. We are working together to try and accomplish your dream. You are inspiring us in this work. We pray that by your causeless mercy we can realize the ambitious vision you had for the Toronto *yātrā*.

The community of Toronto Vaiṣṇavas is trying to push on your preaching mission. Our community is growing slowly but surely. We are trying to increase our book distribution, *prasādam* distribution, preaching programs, community development, youth initiatives, and general devotional spirit. We sincerely ask that you continue to inspire us; help us to be to be humble, enthusiastic, joyful, sincere, and surrendered. We bow our unworthy heads at your glorious lotus feet again and again.

All glories to His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your servants in New Remuṇā dhāma, Toronto, Canada.*

## New Sīmantadvīpa

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Thank you for all the wonderful gifts and blessings you have given us.

You have given us the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement, and this movement is spreading to many cities and towns in this country, even to the small town where I live.

You have let us know what we should do and what we should not. You have given us this most attractive Vedic philosophy and culture. We all like the beautiful saris and *dhotīs* and all the rest. We know that this is our real life.

You have given us Deity worship and festivals and feasts so that we can easily accept the most profound knowledge of all, knowledge of the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

You have given us our spiritual masters, *dīkṣā* and *śikṣā,* and they are patiently guiding us so we can perfect our existence and develop love for Kṛṣṇa.

You have given us the holy names, which we regularly chant during *japa* and sing loudly in varied transcendental melodies during ecstatic *kīrtanas*. The holy names are the means to achieve *kṛṣṇa-prema*. People who accept the holy names never fear anything again.

Your own singing is especially sweet: the more people listen, the more they are attracted. Every time we listen to your *bhajans,* we remember your face, your mission, and your wishes.

You have made us all into one big harmonious family, with no consideration of who we are or where we come from.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, what more could we ask? You have given us everything we need to go back home, back to Godhead. Now it is up to us to embrace these treasures and take advantage of them to cultivate our Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Yet, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we still have two requests: Please bless that we may remember Kṛṣṇa and serve Kṛṣṇa forever, and please give us more and more devotional service.

Happy Appearance Day. Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

*Your humble servants at New Sīmantadvīpa, Khasadesa.*

## New Tālavan

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you, Srila Prabhupada!

We would also like to offer our humble obeisances unto all the spiritual masters in ISKCON.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our instructing spiritual master. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanya and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism. We are fallen souls, and you wake us up from deep sleep. We get new lives by Kṛṣṇa’s mercy.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the spiritual master of the spiritual masters of all the devotees in New Tālavan. You have given us mercy and opened our eyes with the torch of knowledge. You have made us give up the desire to become masters. Although we still live in the material world, you have made us understand that everything is Kṛṣṇa’s property, that there is only one master: Kṛṣṇa. We are servants of the servants of the servants. By your blessings, we dance and chant in transcendental bliss: Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

*All the devotees in New Tālavan, Khasadeśa.*

## New Tālavan (Mississippi)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Moment to moment, year to year we strive to serve, obey, and please you. Each year we grow in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We hope you are pleased with our progress. We are just waiting for the day to again see and hear you face to face.

Whatever we know about the lord is a result of devoting ourselves to your orders and service. You made us the most fortunate, and we are feeling more and more grateful. The lifestyle you taught us and the books you gave us are enabling us to transcend the *māyā* of this material nature. We owe you an unpayable debt because you have given us the opportunity to become most fortunate. A fortune beyond material opulence. A fortune full of transcendental bliss and goodness. A fortune that is blooming more and more, creating fragrant blossoms of *kṛṣṇa-prema*. It feels so good to be indebted to you, which is just the opposite of owing anyone anything in this material world.

Our hope it to grow up and be strong like you so we can help people as you helped us. May we achieve just a fraction of your success. Please bless us to never leave your service and to always remain a part of your transcendental entourage. Let us help make ISKCON the organization you desired it to be. May we acquire the vision and determination to make it happen for ourselves, ISKCON, and all humanity. May we continue to strive to reach the goals we set.

Begging to remain with the devotees fixed at your lotus feet,

*Your eternal servants at ISKCON in Mississippi, USA.*

## New Vraja (Khasadeśa)

Our dearest grandfather Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you!

We in New Vraja *dhāma* are greatly fortune to have the association of your pure devotees. They are all your disciples or the disciples of your disciples. From the association with these elevated souls, we can feel your causeless mercy. They carry your message to this hopeless land, which is like a desert in the material world. How fortunate we are to be in touch with you through your representatives! You have empowered your servants to establish devotional service to Lord Caitanya in our hearts.

Simply by the mercy of pure devotees of the Lord, like your wonderful disciples, we have had the good fortune to become part of the Caitanya tree.

*vande śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya-premāmara-taroḥ priyān*  
*śākhā-rūpān bhakta-gaṇān kṛṣṇa-prema-phala-pradān*

**“I offer my obeisances to all the devotees of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, the eternal tree of love of Godhead. I offer my respects to all the branches of the tree, the devotees of the Lord who distribute the fruit of love of Kṛṣṇa.” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi*** **10.7)**

We cannot talk about this tree without talking about ISKCON. As you have said, ISKCON is your body. In the purport to this verse, you say, “ISKCON is one of these branches \[of the Caitanya tree\], and it should therefore be respected by all sincere devotees of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu.”

For the devotees in New Vraja, the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, ISKCON, and you are identical. You have spread this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement across the entire world through ISKCON, and thus Lord Caitanya’s prophecy that the holy name will be preached in every town and village is being fulfilled.

And when we go to Śrī Vṛndāvana and Śrī Māyāpur, we see even more clearly how youthful and healthy ISKCON is. When we chant the holy name with your other children and grandchildren from all over the world, we are amazed. Everything about Kṛṣṇa consciousness is gradually being revealed to us through the mercy of your representatives. Therefore it is mentioned in *Śrī Gurv-aṣṭaka:*

*sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstrair*  
*uktas tathā bhāvyata eva sadbhiḥ*  
*kintu prabhor yaḥ priya eva tasya*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**“The spiritual master is to be honored as much as the Supreme Lord, because he is the most confidential servitor of the Lord. This is acknowledged in all revealed scriptures and followed by all authorities. Therefore I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master, who is a bona fide representative of Śrī Hari (Kṛṣṇa).”**

You are truly *patita-pāvana,* the deliverer of all fallen souls. We cannot fully express our gratitude to Your Divine Grace. Before becoming servants of the devotees, we were lower than worms in stool, but now that we have obtained the association of your pure devotees, our lives have changed, just as by associating with fire, iron itself also becomes fire.

We are now tools in the hands of our masters, no longer slaves to false ego and false prestige. Now we must strive more and more to dovetail our free will with the sweet will of our masters. What great mercy!

How can we repay our debt to you? We pray that life after life we may render devotional service to your servants and the servants of your servants. We pray that in our humble endeavor to please you, we may somehow or other begin to understand your causeless mercy and your endless glories and that we may pass that mercy and those glories on and on so that more and more conditioned souls will surrender to your pure representatives and come to live in ISKCON, our eternal home coming directly from the spiritual sky.

Please accept our most humble obeisances unto your lotus feet again and again.

May the chanting of the Lord’s holy name reverberate through the whole of Khasadeśa!

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

*Your most humble grandchildren in New Vraja, Khasadeśa.*

## New Vraja-dhāma (Hungary)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

We bow down before you on this blessed day, the day of your divine appearance, begging for your blessings so that the flower garland of our words can become your worthy ornament reflecting your extraordinary greatness for the entire world.

Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura writes in one of His beautiful poems that we can never lose the association of Vaiṣṇavas for they live forever through their *vāṇī,* their instructions:

He reasons ill who tells that Vaiṣṇavas die  
When thou art living still in sound!  
The Vaiṣṇavas die to live, and living try  
To spread the holy name around!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we never had the chance to see you personally or to attend your classes given in different temples of the world. We could never join you for your morning walks and never went to airports with happy excited hearts to wait for you to arrive and be greeted by your loving disciples. Yet, by your endless mercy we are fully able to get your personal association if we read your transcendental books, through which you directly teach us every day; if we very strictly and attentively follow your disciples who brought your instructions to us in their hearts, making it possible for us to share in the sweet nectar of your divine association. Like a precious lotus bud, the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement blossomed forth from the core of your heart and intoxicated the entire world. “Everything is possible by the desire of a pure devotee.”

Dear Prabhupāda, it was by your desire that Kṛṣṇa consciousness spread all over the world. It was by your hard work that countless temples opened, where devotees can worship the divine forms of Kṛṣṇa day by day. It is due to your endless mercy that Vaiṣṇavas can distribute your wonderful books and sing the holy names for their own delight and that of all conditioned souls. With the blessings of Śrī Kṛṣṇa you established the matchless institution of ISKCON, which is like a huge house where your followers can live as a big family, keeping Kṛṣṇa in the center of their lives.

From your mercy appeared various farm communities as the pillars of the *varṇāśrama* system. The goal of these communities is to provide shelter from the culture of the Western world. New Vraja-dhāma was established for this reason ten years ago, and by your blessings it continues to develop dynamically.

Thank you, dear Prabhupāda, for living among us eternally. The company of your loving disciples, the reading and distribution of your priceless books, the chanting of the holy name, the service of the beautiful forms of Rādhā-Śyāmasundara—all reflect your divine greatness, for you brought all these to us. The world would be empty without them, and so would the hearts of all the suffering souls.

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda! May your fame be spread throughout the three worlds.

*Your servants in New Vraja-dhāma, Hungary.*

## New Vrindaban

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda

Please accept our humble obeisances.

You sacrificed your life giving the world gifts to purify our hearts. You created an organization to assist searching souls. You built temples to facilitate the worship of Lord Kṛṣṇa. You personally instructed thousands of disciples through letters and lectures. All the while, you tirelessly translated volumes of authorized Vedic literatures and presented your realized conclusions meant to enlighten and inspire human society for thousands of years in Kali-yuga.

Five thousand years after Lord Kṛṣṇa, five hundred years after Lord Caitanya, you appeared on the planet to spread Their message and continue Their *līlā* by attracting fallen souls from an uncivilized society. We have traveled through millions of lives in millions of species of life in millions of universes to receive the rare, causeless opportunity to associate and serve the *paramparā* lineage of Lord Kṛṣṇa through you, the empowered pure devotee.

*Brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva/ guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*. You planted the seeds of devotion in the hearts of your followers. If we hope to receive your continued mercy, we must show Lord Kṛṣṇa that we hold, as most valuable, your gifts to the world.

In our Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement we inaugurated the New Vṛndāvana activities, wherein devotees are always engaged in the transcendental loving service of the Lord, and this is not different from Goloka Vṛndāvana. \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 7.69, purport\].

This will be our humble contribution to your ISKCON mission, and hopefully our chance to qualify for your next mission. You said, “Work now, *samādhi* later.” You were the living example of that statement. Your *samādhi* was to dedicate every moment of your life to serving the lost souls of Kali-yuga. Unqualified and sinful, we still pray that by serving those carrying on your mission, we might gain your association in our next life. May we follow you to your next mission, your next steamship trip to the next New York, in the next universe. While this may not be the traditional picture of *samādhi,* it is the *samādhi* of the *ācārya*.

Dear Lord Kṛṣṇa, please hear our prayer and grant our wish—that we may take birth wherever your topmost preacher, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is revealing Your glories.

*Your servants in the New Vrindaban community, West Virginia, USA.*

## New York (26 Second Avenue)

**These Days at Matchless Gifts**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

We send this offering of our love for you at a time of extreme turbulence. For the first time in its history the United States has, without first being attacked, deployed an invading army against another nation. A failing economy has led to the highest unemployment in decades. Hate crimes are on the rise—the list of woes goes on and on, and none of it surprises us. There are no pretty stories in Kali’s age.

What does surprise us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is the efficacy of your teachings on scales large and small. What you have provided the world takes our breath away. We see tangibly what despots and power brokers fail to understand: that lasting history is made not by explosive external forces but by steady increments of internal growth.

Our focus here at your first temple in America remains on the individual, on building a solid team of well-educated, committed devotees. And Kṛṣṇa has kindly sent so many! The growth of activity here at 26 Second Avenue is not cataclysmic, neither in size nor scope, but it is steady.

We take to heart the preaching spirit that you personified, Śrīla Prabhupāda: you attracted people to Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet by your example of humility, devotion, and knowledge. These days when someone leaves our company, we don’t blame Māyā. We search our hearts to see what we may have done to prompt their departure. We ask Kṛṣṇa for guidance and reach out to help that person return. These days when we distribute books, there is concern for the recipient and an effort to support quantities of books with quality of education. These days, should we confront litigious detractors, we assume responsibility for actions of the past, negotiate remedies, and determine safeguards so that similar friction may not recur.

We concentrate on improving the quality of our chanting. We open our minds to a more mature analysis of your books. We discuss applications of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in a world beset by armed conflict, poverty, and environmental decay. We marvel at how teachings you gave us thirty years ago continue to reveal hidden dimensions of meaning. Your books are living, breathing works that remain relevant to challenges no one could have anticipated when you first arrived in America: globalization; information overload; Internet culture; remote warfare; cloning and other dramatic discoveries in genetics. You gave the world wisdom whose applications are limited only by our own lack of imagination and insight.

We strive to maintain programs you always liked: festivals and *harināma*, *Gītā* classes and cooking courses, feasts and Ratha-yātrā. And we have exhilarating visions of what might one day be: an elegant cultural center in New York City, residential buildings, art projects, and university programs—but we approach these knowing that larger edifices require stronger foundations. Our ambitions have not gone away; they’ve just become more realistic. We must expand our understanding of your teachings or risk seeing Kṛṣṇa consciousness relegated to studies of Sixties religious movements. Our greatest fear, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is that you might ever be marginalized in the history of humanity’s march toward enlightenment.

So we focus on growing our community, your community, of aspiring devotees of the Lord. We think of you always, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and pray for your help in this immense task of evolving your movement to its rightful place of global importance. Bless us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so that we may carry on your work as seasoned and worthy representatives of your mission.

*Your humble servants at the “Matchless Gifts” temple, 26 Second Avenue, New York, New York, USA.*  
(written by Yogeśvara dāsa)

## New York (Brooklyn)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

*asac-ceṣṭā-kaṣṭa-prada-vikaṭa-pāśālibhir iha*  
*prakāmaṁ kāmādi-prakaṭa-pathapāti-vyatikaraiḥ*  
*gale baddhvā hanye 'ham iti baka-bhid vartmapa-gaṇe*  
*kuru tvaṁ phut-kārān avati sa yathā tvaṁ mana itaḥ*

**“The highwaymen of lust and his accomplices—greed, etc.—have waylaid me and bound my neck with the horrible ropes of sinful activities. O mind, please scream out for help, crying ‘O Kṛṣṇa! O killer of Baka, I am on the verge of death!’ If you do this, then Kṛṣṇa will certainly save me.” (*****Śrī Manaḥ-śikṣā*** **5)**

*anyābhilāṣitā-śūnyaṁ jñāna-karmādy-anāvṛtam*  
*ānukūlyena kṛṣṇānu-śīlanaṁ bhaktir uttamā*

**“One should render transcendental loving service to the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa favorably and without desire for material profit or gain through fruitive activities or philosophical speculation. That is called pure devotional service.” (*****Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu*** **1.1.11)**

A simple and sublime instruction from Śrīla Prabhupāda:

The position of *viśuddha-sattva* is the position of uncontaminated goodness. On that platform one can then understand, *ārādhyo bhagavān vrajeśa-tanayas tad-dhāma vṛndāvanam:* “The Supreme Personality of Godhead, the son of Nanda Mahārāja, is to be worshiped along with His transcendental abode, Vṛndāvana.”

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

I am a fool, insignificant, prostrate, worshiping your lotus feet. I am a fool eager to hear you, inquisitive and submissive, guided by your illuminating lotus words. I am a fool traversing the *bhakti* path, though lame, held upright only by the support of inadequate occasional dedication to serve your pure desire. Please direct your mercy our way, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we need your love and inspiration, your empowerment, so we can walk strong on the path back to Godhead.

You taught us to offer our every act to Śrī Govinda, Gopīnātha, and Madana-mohana—simple and sublime teachings to wake us from our perpetual slumber of foolish bliss, *avidyā,* the ruler of mundane consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your expert guidance simple devotional service shelters us and we relish the sweet sublime taste of the burden of love you allow us to bear in the course of serving you, serving the ISKCON institution, and serving all your servants, all true members of the ISKCON *saṅgha* of Vaiṣṇavas. In *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* Lord Kṛṣṇa provides a simple and sublime formula for a spiritual reawakening, the ultimate success in life. With a concise excerpt of your Bhaktivedanta purport to *Bhagavad-gītā* 9.27, we see the simple goal you make our target:

*patraṁ puṣpaṁ phalaṁ toyaṁ yo me bhaktyā prayacchati*  
*tad ahaṁ bhakty-upahṛtam aśnāmi prayatātmanaḥ*

**“If one offers Me with love and devotion a leaf, a flower, a fruit, or water, I will accept it.”**

PURPORT: For the intelligent person, it is essential to be in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, engaged in the transcendental loving service of the Lord, in order to achieve a permanent, blissful abode for eternal happiness. The process of achieving such a marvelous result is very easy and can be attempted even by the poorest of the poor, without any kind of qualification. The only qualification required in this connection is to be a pure devotee of the Lord....

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your mercy is available for all students of your flawless, pure, and potent Bhaktivedanta Purports. We only have to apply those simple directions and follow sincerely, and success is guaranteed. Your unique contribution of Deity worship in ISKCON temples combined with good congregational association, *śravaṇam, kīrtanam,* the chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare is a brilliant development, an easily executable spiritual standard, engaging us in Vaiṣṇava brahminical *dharma* and making us potent capable instruments for assisting in growing this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement throughout society at large. Now we simply have to obey your order, and individual success, as well as the success of ISKCON, is assured.

We pray for your special mercy today, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so we may be sincere in action and serve you with love. It is then only that love, peace, and happiness will abound in our hearts and in the corridors, halls, and gardens of ISKCON and replace the wicked intrusion of lust, anger, avarice, greed, and *aparādha,* which lay in wait like robbers on the highway. We are in want of nothing save and except Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and by your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, Kṛṣṇa consciousness is now abundantly available in the marketplace.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have opened for us the proverbial jar of honey, the sweet intoxicating elixir of Kṛṣṇa conscious bliss. It rewards those sincere souls who submerge deep in that nectar, not drowning them but rather providing eternal life in an ocean of devotion and bliss.

In your purport to *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* 4.35is another illumination you shine on our darkened hearts to dispel our insincere foolishness:

*yaj jñātvā na punar moham evaṁ yāsyasi pāṇḍava*  
*yena bhūtāny aśeṣāṇi drakṣyasy ātmany atho mayi*

**“Having obtained real knowledge from a self-realized soul, you will never fall again into such illusion, for by this knowledge you will see that all living beings are but part of the Supreme, or, in other words, that they are Mine.”**

PURPORT: The result of receiving knowledge from a self-realized soul, or one who knows things as they are, is learning that all living beings are parts and parcels of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa....One can acquire such pure knowledge only from the bona fide spiritual master and thereby avoid the delusion that the living entity is equal to Kṛṣṇa....

We strive to be servants of your desire, cleansing ourselves of material aspiration, a healthy condition of heart. To please you on this auspicious day of Vyāsa-pūjā we commit ourselves to glorifying rather than vilifying, to encouraging rather than discouraging your disciples and grand-disciples. Our determination is to serve you sincerely and serve your servants by assisting them in serving sincerely. Before you on this wonderful day we commit ourselves to striving for loving exchanges of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, to taking your instructions to heart, and to endeavoring for the protection, preservation, and growth of ISKCON as our prime goal.

We want all members of ISKCON to regularly study, apply, and teach the *śikṣā* presented in your wonderful, enlightening Kṛṣṇa consciousness literature.

We want everyone to be happy and wise, and what better assured path to enlightenment and true happiness than to study and practice what you preach to us, your foolish children?

In *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* 18.65 you guide us on the fast track to Lord Kṛṣṇa’s satisfaction, mercy, friendship, and love:

*man-manā bhava mad-bhakto mad-yājī māṁ namaskuru*  
*mām evaiṣyasi satyaṁ te pratijāne priyo 'si me*

**“Always think of Me, become My devotee, worship Me, and offer your homage unto Me. Thus you will come to Me without fail. I promise you this because you are My very dear friend.”**

PURPORT: The most confidential part of knowledge is that one should become a pure devotee of Kṛṣṇa and always think of Him and act for Him. One should not become an official meditator. Life should be so molded that one will always have the chance to think of Kṛṣṇa. One should always act in such a way that all his daily activities are in connection with Kṛṣṇa. He should arrange his life in such a way that throughout the twenty-four hours he cannot but think of Kṛṣṇa. And the Lord’s promise is that anyone who is in such pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness will certainly return to the abode of Kṛṣṇa, where he will be engaged in the association of Kṛṣṇa face to face. This most confidential part of knowledge is spoken to Arjuna because he is the dear friend of Kṛṣṇa. Everyone who follows the path of Arjuna can become a dear friend to Kṛṣṇa and obtain the same perfection as Arjuna....

The value of your teachings is greater than pure gold or flawless precious gems, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You never leave us short of the goal, Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You stress without compromise the real importance of our human life, the real importance of devotional service, *bhakti-yoga,* for Lord Kṛṣṇa’s pleasure: ultimate success, the topmost elevation to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. An eternal, ever-increasing, concerted composition of loving exchange in relationship with the supreme reservoir of all *rasa,* Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda. His kindness brought you to this shore; your kindness leads us to His door. Devotional service, dedication, loyalty, and cooperation to keep ISKCON together kindle our love for you all the more. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for caring for us and guiding us. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for inspiring us to traverse the *bhakti* path of highest approach so freely offered for all sincere servants to be enriched by.

In *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* 18.68, Lord Kṛṣṇa and you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, His beloved pure devotee, direct us to use the proverbial blacksmith’s hammer to break through the walls of Māyā’s fortress and enter our real home, Śrī Vṛndāvana *dhāma:*

*ya idaṁ paramaṁ guhyaṁ mad-bhakteṣv abhidhāsyati*  
*bhaktiṁ mayi parāṁ kṛtvā mām evaiṣyaty asaṁśayaḥ*

**“For one who explains this supreme secret to the devotees, pure devotional service is guaranteed, and at the end he will come back to Me.”**

PURPORT: Generally it is advised that *Bhagavad-gītā* be discussed amongst the devotees only, for those who are not devotees will understand neither Kṛṣṇa nor *Bhagavad-gītā*. Those who do not accept Kṛṣṇa as He is and *Bhagavad-gītā* as it is should not try to explain *Bhagavad-gītā* whimsically and become offenders. *Bhagavad-gītā* should be explained to persons who are ready to accept Kṛṣṇa as the Supreme Personality of Godhead. It is a subject matter for the devotees only and not for philosophical speculators. Anyone, however, who tries sincerely to present *Bhagavad-gītā* as it is will advance in devotional activities and reach the pure devotional state of life. As a result of such pure devotion, he is sure to go back home, back to Godhead.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the pure devotee representative of the Supreme Absolute Truth, the all-attractive, omnipotent, omnipresent, and omniscient speaker of the *Bhagavad-gītā,* Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you know, understand, and love Lord Kṛṣṇa, and He loves you; therefore we choose as our goal the target of unflinching faith in you, our beloved spiritual master, father, and ever well-wisher.

You once said no one can purchase a ticket to Vṛndāvana, and we know by your mercy that we must first deserve, then desire. By your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have the great good fortune of serving Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma by performing our devotional service in any part of the world. Wherever pure devotion to Lord Kṛṣṇa is manifest, that is Vṛndāvana; therefore wherever you are, Śrīla Prabhupāda, wherever your orders are perfectly executed, wherever your disciples dance and chant in ecstasy the *mahā-mantra:* Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare—that is Vṛndāvana. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have arranged by your pure devotion that Vṛndāvana is now available in even the most remote corners of this world. Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for bringing us all back home to Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma.

A final quote for today, this most glorious day, Śrīla Prabhupāda, from the best of songs, the song we all relish and love, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s supreme song, *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is:*

*na ca tasmān manuṣyeṣu kaścin me priya-kṛttamaḥ*  
*bhavitā na ca me tasmād anyaḥ priyataro bhuvi*

**“There is no servant in this world more dear to Me than he, nor will there ever be one more dear.” (*****Bhagavad-gītā*** **18.69)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda you are the servant Lord Kṛṣṇa speaks of in this wonderful verse. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the most dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa. No one, Śrīla Prabhupāda, will ever be more dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa than you, and no one will ever be more dear to us than you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Everyone loves Śrīla Prabhupāda. Śrīla Prabhupāda is our Father.

We pray on this most auspicious day of Vyāsa-pūjā, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please continue to teach us, please continue to uncover the illuminating knowledge of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in our hearts. Please don’t allow us to remain blind, insincere fools, your *aspiring* menial servants, please don’t allow us to remain lame and ill advised, please bless us so we may be your devoted, sincere, and loyal servants, always hankering for your wonderful mercy, which is truth and the light that guides us and illumines the path back to Kṛṣṇa, back to Śrī Vṛndāvana. We pray to remain your menial servants birth after birth.

*Śrīla Prabhupāda’s insignificant, unfortunate, and unqualified beggars from Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda mandira, Brooklyn, New York, USA.*  
(written by Rāmabhadra dāsa)

## Noida

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā occasion, we devotees of ISKCON Noida offer our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

I would first of all like to express my deepest satisfaction at being a member of this ISKCON movement, through which you have offered the entire world a way to live peacefully under the protection of Lord Kṛṣṇa and his devotees.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, frankly speaking, after many years in your movement it is only recently that I came to understand the purpose of ISKCON as you defined it:

To educate all people in the techniques of spiritual life in order to check the imbalance of values in life and to achieve real unity and peace in the world.

This vision of ISKCON is clear, and your sincere disciples and grand-disciples are dedicated to realizing it. So I would like to offer my humble obeisances to all your followers, in whom I see you and your wonderful qualities.

This temple, originally known as Devālaya, is a place where one learns the science of re-establishing one’s eternal relationship with Ādideva (Kṛṣṇa or Śrī Viṣṇu).

The purpose of ISKCON is mainly to enable the general masses to understand the higher goal of life, which is to establish their loving relationship with the prime entity, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. This can be furthered by developing temples that offer a systematic approach to cultivating higher consciousness and that ultimately lead one to developing love of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

ISKCON temples help all its visitors, friends, and congregational members develop a taste for Kṛṣṇa consciousness by providing an opportunity for *darśana* of the beautiful Deities, for hearing about Him, for chanting His holy name, for reading His instructions as revealed in *Śrīmad Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, for relishing foods offered to Him, and for associating with His devotees, who are living examples of purity and knowledge. Many who partake of these opportunities are inspired to render practical service according to their capacity. Thus the spiritual needs of the society are met by ISKCON temples.

Here is a humble report of ISKCON Noida’s project, the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda Mandir and Bhaktivedanta Vidyāpīṭha We are extremely pleased to inform you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that ISKCON Noida has been allotted a prime piece of land in heart of the city to erect the temple. There is also a vast piece of land behind the proposed temple premises, which we plan to develop into a Kṛṣṇa Jayanti park. The land belongs to the city, and we plan to obtain the necessary permits to develop the park. It would be an ideal place for spiritual picnics for the families of Noida, a facility the city lacks at present.

The planned five-storied temple will be a beautiful piece of modern architecture blended with the ethnic look of a traditional temple. The various floors of the temple will be used as follows:

The basement will accommodate a multipurpose hall of about eight thousand square feet. A stage with attached green rooms will host festive programs. The hall will also be used as *saṁskara-bhavana* and occasionally for distributing *prasādam* at big festivals. Intercollegiate competitions and other socio-spiritual programs like *bhāgavata-kathā* will be conducted in this hall.

The ground floor will have a marketplace with a booth for books, paraphernalia, flowers, gifts, etc. A Govinda’s restaurant will offer the most sumptuous *kṛṣṇa-prasādam*. On this floor there will also be administrative offices and living quarters for resident teachers and devotees.

The first floor will be totally dedicated to the cause of propagating of higher education through the Bhaktivedanta Vidyāpīṭha. It will house five classrooms, a Vedic audio & video library, and a seminar and symposium hall that will accommodate senior teachers and worldwide spiritual leaders when they come to conduct courses.

The second floor will house the temple room, with its huge, beautifully decorated *darśana-maṇḍapa, garbha-graha,* Garuḍa-stambha, and a diorama gallery at mezzanine level. These dioramas will depict the pastimes and teachings of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, Rāma, and Caitanya Mahāprabhu. The project is schedule to be completed Gītā-jayanti of 2005.

Under the guidance of His Holiness Lokanāth Swami and His Holiness Gopāl Kṛṣṇa Goswami, the team of devotees consisting of Vaṁśīdhara dāsa, Vaiṣṇava dāsa, Harikīrtana dāsa, Sanātana dāsa, Ajāmila dāsa, Ātmārāma dāsa, Trilokanātha dāsa, Ramākānta dāsa, Valab Prasad Aggarval, R. N. Guptaji, Manish Guptaji, Mohan Ahluwalia, and Manish Ahuluwaia, along with the entire ISKCON Noida congregation, desperately request you to kindly shower your mercy and bless this project. We are confident that by your causeless mercy the ISKCON Noida project will get built on time. Your mercy and potency are inconceivable.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless me so that I can serve you and your ISKCON movement along with the other devotees under the guidance of my spiritual master.

*Your servants in Noida, India.*  
(written by Vraja-bhakti-vilāsa dāsa)

## Ottawa

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories unto you!

On the auspicious occasion of the anniversary of your divine appearance, I wish to express my sincere thanks and gratitude unto you. One hundred and seven years ago, you came from the spiritual abode to perform the most endearing service to your Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who says in the *Bhagavad-gītā* (18.68–69):

For one who explains this supreme secret to the devotees, pure devotional service is guaranteed, and at the end he will come back to Me. There is no servant in this world more dear to Me than he, nor will there ever be one more dear.

Although you are eternally situated in transcendental loving service to your Lord, still, as you wrote in this purport to Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura’s song *Hari Hari Biphale*:

This Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, *saṅkīrtana* movement, is nothing material. It is directly imported from the spiritual kingdom known as Goloka Vṛndāvana. So golokera *prema-dhana*. And it is not ordinary song. It is just the treasure of love of Godhead. So... “But I have no attraction for this.” *Rati nā janmilo kene tāy*. “I have no attraction for it. On the contrary,” *viṣaya-biṣānale, dibā-niśi hiyā jwale, “*and because I did not accept it, therefore the blazing fire of the poison of material existence is constantly burning me.” *Dibā-niśi hiyā jwale*. “Day and night, my heart is burning on account of this poisonous effect of material existence.” And *taribare nā koinu upāy*. “But I did not seek any remedy for this.” In other words, the remedy for this blazing fire of material existence is this *saṅkīrtana* movement. It is imported from the spiritual kingdom. And who has imported it? Or who has brought it?

Then he says, *brajendra-nandana jei, śaci-suta hoilo sei*. Brajendra-nandana, the son of King of Braja. That is Kṛṣṇa. Kṛṣṇa is known as son of Nanda Mahārāja. He was King of Brajabhūmi. So *brajendra-nandana jei,* the same personality who was formerly the son of Nanda Mahārāja has now appeared as the son of Mother Śacī. *Śacī-suta hoilo sei*. And *balarāma hoilo nitāi*. And Lord Balarāma has become Nityānanda. So these two brothers adventing, they are delivering all kinds of fallen souls. *Pāpī-tāpī jata chilo*. As many fallen souls there were within this world, they’re delivering them simply by this chanting procession. *Hari-nāme uddhārilo,* simply by this chanting.

You also have nothing to do with this material world. But just as the Lord came in His *nāma-rūpa* directly from Goloka Vṛndāvana, the Lord, in the mood of His topmost devotee, Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, came as Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. He came to demonstrate and propagate *harināma-saṅkīrtana,* along with His associates.

You once stated that Lord Caitanya established *harināma-saṅkīrtana* within India, but He left the rest of the world for you and your ISKCON. The Lord—actually both Lords, Gaura and Nitāi—especially empowered you and brought you from Vṛndāvana to spread this *harināma-saṅkīrtana,* the only remedy for extinguishing our unlimited material desires. You successfully spread *harināma-saṅkīrtana* throughout the rest of the world all by yourself, under the order of your beloved spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you literally sacrificed your life to give the world this *harināma-saṅkīrtana*. On this auspicious occasion, we humbly pray that we can properly learn our ABC’s under your expert and kind guidance:

**A—A**lways seek the association of your advanced servants and serve them with the proper attitude.

**B—B**e determined to perform all our activities on the basis of your Bhaktivedanta books, lectures, letters, etc.

**C—C**onstantly chant the holy names of the Lord given by Lord Caitanya and taught by you.

*Harināma-saṅkīrtana kī jaya!* Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

*Your humble servants and students in Ottawa, Canada.*  
(written by Śaṅkara dāsa)

## Panama

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.”**

**“Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Would be good if you can be with us still.  
The light of the day will shine more,  
and our strength for intense preaching will be immense,  
and to face adversities.  
The riotous allowance of the rampant senses  
sends the people from there to here,  
and from here to ungrateful distances,  
like drunken sleepwalkers,  
alienated inside and without calculated course,  
and the miseries asphyxiate.  
The war spreads on great scale,  
caused by dark interests,  
and illusion resembles a confinement without exit.  
From one side to the other one, the anguish  
of the desperate ones is perceived.  
Many are turned aside by wannabe religious people  
that fill their pockets  
with their forced monthly donations.  
Spreading Lord Caitanya’s mercy is as urgent  
as air and water are for life.  
But the devotees—we are weak and full of doubts frequently,  
and sometimes we are assaulted by confusion,  
and Māyā reigns.  
So good that you will be still with us...Still...  
Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Yes!  
You are present in your books and teachings...!  
But...Still we need your severe and sweet look,  
your smile, your enthusiasm, your breath!  
O Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Give us your blessings every day to keep on going,  
Preaching...  
and back to Godhead.  
So good that you will be still with us.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda.

With all love and gratitude,  
*Your servants in Panama.*  
(written by Hari Cakra dāsa)

## Pandharpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances. All glories to you on your most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day.

We are very fortunate that you have given us a chance to render service unto the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Here in the holy *dhāma* of Pandharpur, all of us from different places around India and the world have the opportunity to render services. Because of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, centers all around the world have been opened to give us conditioned souls a chance to associate with each other and Kṛṣṇa.

Here at Pandharpur our center is called Hare Kṛṣṇa Dhāma. We are living in Hare Kṛṣṇa Dhāma at Pandharpur Dhāma. We are fortunate in that situation. At the same time, we are trying our best to preach your glories and the mission of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu to all the pilgrims who come to Pandharpur Dhāma.

We have one of your generals, His Holiness Lokanāth Mahārāja, as our leader. He passes your mercy on to us and inspires is to try to push on ISKCON’s preaching mission. The situation is getting better. Now we are fifteen devotees. We are building a new *goshala* and have just completed a multipurpose hall. On Śrī Rādhāṣṭamī this year we hope to lay down the cornerstone for our Śrī Śrī Rādhā Pandarināth Temple. With your mercy we hope to complete the new temple by Rādhāṣṭamī 2004.

There is also a plan to erect a *pandal* on the other side of the Candrabhaga River to increase our book distribution and preaching.

Another preaching project springing forth from ISKCON Pandharpur is ISKCON Padayātrā Maharashtra. We are in the process of starting a permanent Padayātrā in Maharashtra.

We hope to please you and get your blessings by these new developments in ISKCON Pandharpur.

Always hoping to remain servants at your lotus feet,  
*The devotees of ISKCON Pandharpur, India.*

## Paris

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble and respectful obeisances on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā. I bow to your lotus feet and beg for your never-ending mercy to allow me to continue to serve your movement somehow or other. This writing of a Vyāsa-pūjā offering is most difficult when I see so many learned and pure devotees endeavoring with all their hearts to serve you in great separation.

*Voilṁ,* one of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s miracles: even a lowborn prostitute can become elevated to chant the holy names and serve the Lord. We are celebrating this year the thirtieth anniversary of the installation of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara, and for Their pleasure the devotees of the Paris *yātrā* would like to offer Them a new temple. May you bless them so that they can successfully transfer the Deities into Their new home and thereby revive our enthusiasm to serve Them. May you please also take your place on the *vyāsāsana* and guide our efforts to serve your instructions.

We need you more than ever, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We need your inspiration, your strength, your powerful boldness in preaching.

May you please enter our hearts, setting them ablaze. May we also understand the transcendental nature of your personality so we can better serve you in transcendental separation. May we deliver your transcendental message, which shows people how to reach the perfection of the human form of life.

We also pray to become humble and tolerant by accepting our faults and shortcomings. Who are we to criticize one another? Without you we are nothing, or even worse.

You once told Bhagavān dāsa that one day I might render some tangible service to this movement. I pray that that day may come soon. The inconceivable mercy of the spiritual master enables us to inspire those we meet, so I pray that we will all inspire one another and that the tree of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness will blossom and bear its real fruit: pure love of God.

Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your eternal servants in Paris, France.*  
(written by Śatarūpa-devī dāsī)

## Pecs

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my respectful obeisances. All glories to you on this auspicious day!

Today we can celebrate your appearance again. We are fortunate, because we can read about your life. Although you are not present physically, we can get your association through your books and lectures, and through books about you. Your disciples are teaching us, giving us what they received from you. Important rules, philosophy, and love—everything that can take us home, back to Godhead. Everything we receive, and the success we achieve by following, increase the treasure house of your glory.

Kṛṣṇa is known as the most famous personality, but His devotees are even more famous because they speak about His glories. And because you spread Kṛṣṇa’s glories more widely than anyone else, you are the most famous of all, Śrīla Prabhupāda. I would like to tell a little story about how famous you are.

It happened during *saṅkīrtana* here in Hungary, in a small city still affected by remnants of communism. A *mātājī* was going from door to door, and at one house a man opened the door. They started talking, and the man bought a book. He was leafing through the book when he suddenly saw your picture in the back. The man started shouting, “I know this man! I saw him in Central Park in New York in 1972. He was singing there with some young people, and I was there with them. Wait!”

Of course, this was not an ordinary thing, so the *mātājīs* waited with great curiosity. After a few seconds the man came back with a piece of paper. It was a worn leaflet from 1972 with a map showing how to reach the New York temple, and on the other side was the *mahā-mantra*. The man started chanting the *mahā-mantra* spontaneously. He was very happy, and he said that at the time he was a hippie and he was looking for something.

This man found you, Śrīla Prabhupāda—or more precisely we should say that you found him.

Now our duty is to give your association to everyone. We pray that we will be strong enough to become good instruments in our spiritual master’s hands. In this way your glories will be spread all over the world, even to remote places like this small city.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your servants in Pecs, Hungary.*  
(written by Līlārāṇī-devī dāsī)

## Perth

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace and to your faithful followers!

It is a great honor to have the opportunity to include our homage in your Vyāsa-pūjā book. You would receive with pleasure the writings of your disciples on this auspicious day and have them read to you during the ceremony. You would write back to some of them to thank them for their poems and gifts, and would offer your special blessings on this occasion. This is recorded in one of your letters:

Please offer my Vyasa puja blessings to every one of you. I am so much pleased with the book put together by you. It will be read tomorrow in the meeting. Similar publication was done by ISKCON Press. So I thank you very much, all of you, for appreciating my humble service which I am trying to render as a matter of duty ordered by my Guru Maharaja. I request all my disciples to work cooperatively and I am sure our mission will advance without any doubt. \[Letter to Tamāl Krishna Goswami, 14 August 1971\]

We are sure that you still do the same, and we hope that this humble offering pleases you to some extent.

Your Divine Grace visited Perth in May of 1975 and stayed for twelve days. Although there weren’t any big programs, the few devotees assisting you mostly tried to introduce some newcomers to you so they could be purified by hearing transcendental knowledge from your lotus mouth. Your apparently brief visit has purified this city immensely, and slowly but surely the results are being manifested.

After many years of preaching here, your followers have established a nice ISKCON center where their Lordships Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi are being worshiped. We are fortunate enough to have regular preaching and temple programs. There are a considerable number of devotees; some are very new and some are your direct disciples, but everyone has a strong sense of belonging to your transcendental family. By your grace, devotees from different parts of the world and from different backgrounds are able to cooperate within your ISKCON and be sheltered by your compassionate influence.

All the auspicious things that are manifested here are proof of your powerful desire to benefit this world with Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Today in Perth, people are blessed in different ways by contacting your transcendental weapons. Please allow us to tell Your Divine Grace about some of them:

At the temple restaurant right in the middle of the city, hundreds honor *prasādam* weekly. A *harināma* party goes out twice a week to bless the public with the holy names of the Lord. Your books are sold steadily by some very surrendered devotees. We have also been able to hold Ratha-yātrā in grand fashion—as you like it. Lord Jagannātha, Śrīmatī Subhadrā, and Lord Baladeva have been invited to participate in various parades on their chariot, where hundreds of thousands were able to see their Lordships. Even the local TV station broadcast the procession of the devotees chanting in bliss for their Lords. Also, there are many families who practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness at home, cooperating with your temple as much as they can.

Our center is regularly visited by many of your *sannyāsīs* and other senior preachers, who kindly offer their support to those who take shelter in your movement. Some of your senior disciples are living here, blessing the community of devotees with their association and example of “how to stick to Kṛṣṇa consciousness.”

As Your Divine Grace can see, you are very much present in this place through your orders, your representatives, your books, and your devotees.

We thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for coming into our lives and allowing us to be engaged in your mission and to associate with those who follow your instructions. Please bless this group of devotees with your special Vyāsa-pūjā blessings so that we can understand that we are insignificant and that everything is the mercy of Guru, Kṛṣṇa, and the devotees, and so that we can take seriously the words of the spiritual master “as a matter of duty,” and, finally, so that we can work cooperatively to help spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness. In this way your mission will advance without any doubt.

*Your humble servants at Śrī-Śrī Gaura-Nitāi mandira in Perth, Australia.*

## Philadelphia

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this auspicious day of your appearance, we beg to offer our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and to your expertise in time and circumstance! At the height of the hippie movement, you journeyed to America at a time when its youth were searching for God in all the wrong places. Being undaunted by our abominable condition, you patiently guided us as a loving father instructs his small children. You tirelessly traveled from one continent to another, knowing our need for your personal association. By allowing us this time with you, you taught us how to please Lord Kṛṣṇa by properly worshiping the spiritual master. All glories to your unlimited kindness!

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and to your expertise in disseminating transcendental knowledge! By your desire to give us Kṛṣṇa you gave us sight while we were blinded by the fleeting material happiness found within the dense darkness of this world. Not only were you were determined to raise our behavior to that of “ladies and gentlemen,” but you were steadfast in your desire to elevate us to the position of *bhaktas*. In general, people are impressed when a religious personality miraculously heals the sick or disabled. But, you, our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, unassumingly performed thousands of miracles by transforming thousands of sinful youth into aspiring devotees of the Lord. Being resolute in your purpose, you administered the medicine of the holy names. All glories to your unlimited determination!

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who exemplified all of the qualities of a pure devotee of the Lord! By your example you taught us how to live meaningful lives, and in the end you showed us how to fearlessly leave this world. With unending compassion for those left behind, you paved a path to the spiritual kingdom by translating volumes of Vedic literature to guide us along the way. You provided us with a transcendental map to escape the misfortunes of this world. You assured us that if we continue to follow your instructions in your physical absence, we would once again be seated at your lotus feet. With this hope we continue to stumble in our attempt to serve you.

If we sang of your glories for thousands of years, that would still not be enough time to thank you for the precious gift you gave us. As this Age of Kali progresses and Kṛṣṇa consciousness offers solace to more and more souls, it is you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who will be acknowledged as the pure Vaiṣṇava who sacrificed so much to begin this ISKCON movement yet asked so little in return. We pray that we can remain dedicated followers of Your Divine Grace so that we may gain your blessings and the great benediction of carrying on the worldwide movement you worked so hard to build.

Begging to always remain within the cooling shade of your lotus feet,

*Your disciples, grand-disciples, and congregational members at ISKCON Philadelphia, Pennsylvania, USA.*

## Pune

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

The *Śrī Īśopaniṣad* (Mantra 9) states, “Those who engage in the culture of nescient activities shall enter into the darkest region of ignorance. Worse still are those engaged in the culture of so-called knowledge.” In the purport you comment:

*Avidyā,* or ignorance, is undoubtedly dangerous, *but vidyā,* or knowledge, is even more dangerous when mistaken or misguided. This mantra of *Śrī Īśopaniṣad* is more applicable today than at any time in the past. Modern civilization has advanced considerably in the field of mass education, but the result is that people are more unhappy than ever before because of the stress placed on material advancement to the exclusion of the most important part of life, the spiritual aspect.

In a world of cheaters and cheated, you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the fearless champion of truth. You tirelessly preached the pure message of genuine spirituality and thus saved thousands of souls from being misled by modern education and culture.

Pune, known by many as “the Oxford of the East” due to its numerous colleges and universities, is witness to this causeless mercy of yours. Thousands of students from all over India come here to be “spiritually slaughtered” by so-called scientific education and materialistic culture. By your mercy, a transcendental rescue operation of providing real spiritual education to these unfortunate souls was started several years ago. And it has met with significant success. Next to the major colleges of Pune, we now have four Bhaktivedanta Academies for Culture and Education (BACE)—spiritual oases in the desert of material education. In these academies, multitudes of sincere college students practice spiritual life almost to the temple standard and assist the preachers in delivering the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the student community. Spiritual study groups also train over a hundred student-leaders to study your books systematically and to present the message effectively. In addition to the college preaching, organized preaching to children, teenagers, corporate executives, and congregational members has also been expanding. A Bhakti Shāstrī course is going on for the *brahmacārīs* to enable them to dive deeper into your fathomless purports.

This year you also blessed us by giving us the association of many of your senior disciples, who deeply enthused us to push forward your mission of compassion more vigorously.

On this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we pray at your lotus feet for your mercy so that we may become instruments of your compassion to keep spreading the message of Godhead.

*Your servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjabihārī Mandir, Pune, India.*

## KwaZulu Natal

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhānātha!

Over the last few years, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhānātha have created many opportunities for Their devotees to serve Them, spread the message of Lord Caitanyadeva, and advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. By your grace we have progressed in some areas, but in some, due to our neglect of your grace, we have failed.

Again so many exciting opportunities and challenges are before us here at the ISKCON KwaZulu *yātrā*. Lurking nearby are our minds, alluring us with all sorts of solutions, ideas, and plans to realize the opportunities and to overcome the challenges. However, *mahājano yena gataḥ sa panthāḥ*: one should accept whatever progressive path the *mahājanas* advocate.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your example, your instructions, your legacy-you-are the resting place of the *mahājanas*. One simply needs to follow you, learn about you, and, as you said, "Do as I am doing" to favorably please Lord Kṛṣṇa in this modern age.

Let us endeavor to do just that. Let us try to chant purely and humbly, without the incentive of impressing others; let us try to serve without a desire for recognition and position; let us read your books in a prayerful mood, trying not to establish ourselves as scholars but to purify ourselves and attract people to Lord Kṛṣṇa's lotus feet; let us adopt all the avenues of purification you have blessed us with, with a humble disposition. Then we can "simply" follow you.

Today we pledge to do that.

All glories to Your Divine Grace  
*Your servants at ISKCON KwaZulu Natal, South Africa.*

## Rāḍhadesh

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisance at your lotus feet.

On this most auspicious day of your appearance, allow us to write you a few words from your Rāḍhadesh community.

Your presence is felt in your teachings, devotional songs, lectures, images, and *mūrtis;* all are ever-increasingly available through books, audiocassettes, compact disks, videocassettes, digital videodisks, photos, posters, CD ROMS, DVD ROMS, etc. It is impossible to say that your presence is obscured because of your departure, since, due to good use of modern technology, devotees are allowing millions of persons now—and in the future—to experience your presence in many different ways. Besides all these means by which we can associate with you, we also have the blissful association of devotees who are carrying on your mission and fulfilling your desires. We also cherish the proximity of those devotees who had the great good fortune to serve you during your physical presence. They make their memories come alive by sharing them with all those who didn’t have the good fortune to meet you at that time.

Here in Rāḍhadesh we are trying to fulfill your desire to establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness as a solid spiritual alternative for an overwhelmingly materialistic society by developing a thriving spiritual community. Despite our shortcomings and weaknesses, we are enthusiastic in our efforts to please you. I will mention some of the achievements we have accomplished by your mercy and for your pleasure:

Because of a dedicated head *pūjārī* and a steady *pūjārī* team, we have been able to maintain an opulent standard of worship for Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha. A committed management team works cooperatively with our wonderful temple president. Rāḍhadesh’s financial situation is stable.

Along with many ongoing services and preaching programs, including a traveling book distribution party, we have, over the years, been able to make many improvements to Their Lordships’ Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha’s property. Most recently we completed expansions to the cafeteria, restaurant, boutique, and bakery. In addition, the new community hall is finally completed, the finishing touch being intricate wall paintings of kṛṣṇa-*līlā* and *caitanya-līlā*. The new guesthouse is fully functional, even while we continue to work on various fine points of its construction. Much of our building and maintenance needs can be met in the Rāḍhadesh Warehouse and Workshop. Śrīmatī Tulasī Devī now has a first-class greenhouse.

This year we are hosting the International Bhaktivedanta College and Library. The college is organizing Bhakti-śāstrī and ministerial courses to train and educate devotees in the philosophy and practices of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, as well as in other important subjects. This college project has done much to contribute to the Kṛṣṇa conscious atmosphere of our community. We are working to increase our community spirit with social programs such as *iṣṭa-goṣṭhīs* and cultural evenings.

As far as our weaknesses are concerned, we are aware that book distribution needs to be increased. That is the basis of our progressive march in your service. And for maintaining your Rāḍhadesh project and insuring its stability and future service to your mission, we need to develop a Rāḍhadesh constitution. And more than anything, we need and desire to strengthen our interpersonal relationships based on dedication to fulfilling your desire to extend the mercy of Lord Caitanya to the world.

We pray to the Lord that we will always remain at your lotus feet by strictly following your instructions and never deviating from them. Let us make you proud and help you fulfill your mission. Our happiness is to see you satisfied, and we can only beg to continue to receive your blessings year after year.

*Your servants at Rāḍhadesh, Belgium.*

## Richmond Hill/Markham

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is time for the entire world to see  
That you came to earth to set us free.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have awakened us and made us see  
That Kṛṣṇa nectar is even desired by the honeybee.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is time for the entire world to see  
That you are the most bona fide, pure devotee.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have awakened us and made us see  
That it is from Māyā’s grip that we must flee.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is time for the entire world to see  
That Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī and the astrologers did accurately foresee.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have awakened us and made us see  
That we should worship Lord Caitanya, who was born near a Neem tree.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is time for the entire world to see  
That the path to Godhead is not hodgepodge and a potpourri.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have awakened us and made us see  
That we need to concentrate on *kṛṣṇa-bhakti*.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is time for the entire world to see  
That you are the ultimate example of purity.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have awakened us and made us see  
That the ultimate goal of life is not possessions and money.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is time for the entire world to see

How, to preach, you circled the globe twelve times personally.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have awakened us and made us see  
That the connection to Kṛṣṇa is through you, O Gurujī.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is time for the entire world to see  
That building temples around the world was your destiny.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have awakened us and made us see  
That we must forever thank you, A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami.

That you left India to help the fallen souls at the age of seventy.

That spiritual satisfaction comes from devotional service such as cooking and *ārati*.

That by your work devotees are being made from San Francisco to Sydney.

That for spiritual benefit we should chant the *mahā-mantra* with frequency.

That everyone should chant and be happy.

That by your kindness we are able to serve Kṛṣṇa and Rādhārāṇī.

How you tried to preach at every opportunity.

That it is our duty to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness to everybody.

That we are not to follow the instructions of any Māyāvādī.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is time for the entire world to see  
How even with your success you showed so much humility.

How to distinguish between false teachings and reality.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is time for the entire world to see  
That you are the present-age touchstone, *cintāmaṇi*.

How to live like a lotus flower in this materialistic society.

That it is in Kṛṣṇa that they should put their faith completely.

That you designed each individual’s service based on talent and ability.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is time for the entire world to see  
That you successfully converted spiritual vision to actuality.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have awakened us and made us see  
That we should treat everyone respectfully.
