Although we can never repay you, we ask to offer whatever service we can render at your lotus feet in order to help you fulfill your mission and promise to your Guru Mahārāja, His Divine Grace Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura.

Therefore, one year after the inception of Vaiṣṇavas C.A.R.E. (Counseling, Assistance, Resource, and Education for terminally ill and their families) we humbly present this new project in ISKCON as an offering to Your Divine Grace. In order to assist those devotees who are facing imminent death from a terminal illness, we developed this project with the blessings of so many of your senior disciples, such as His Holiness Girirāj Swami and His Holiness Ravīndra Svarūpa Prabhu. His Holiness Bhakti Tīrtha Swami serves on the board of directors and has encouraged many of his disciples to become involved, and for that we are very grateful. At this time we are pleased to report to Your Divine Grace that many Vaiṣṇavas around the world have already volunteered and stand ready and more than willing to help those in a hospice situation if and when the need arises in their own communities. Our goal is to educate volunteers in “every town and village” so no devotee will ever have to leave his or her body without the association of other Vaiṣṇavas and without receiving the gift of the chanting of the holy names of the Lord. We do not take any credit for developing this program, but learned from you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the art of compassion and care for others. In this way, we pray that you are pleased with this project and will bless us so we can continue to care for those devotees who have devoted their lives to spreading your worldwide mission.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who continue to teach us so much about caring for the souls in this world.

*Your humble servants at Vaiṣṇavas C.A.R.E. (Counseling, Assistance, Resources, and Education for terminally ill devotees and their families).*

## Vedic Academy of Spiritual Technology

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are elated to inform you that during the last four months we, a group of devotees from Mumbai, India, under the leadership of your dear disciple His Holiness Śrīdhara Swami, have joined together and formed an organization called the Vedic Academy of Spiritual Technology (VAST) under the ISKCON umbrella to help fulfill your desire of preaching the philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the intelligentsia around the world.

Our Vision and Mission are as follows:

**Vision**

To be recognized as the best provider of spiritual technology in the fields of management and education.

**Mission**

(1) VAST is committed to making a positive difference in educational institutions and at the workplace through professionally supported programs.

(2) VAST will provide spiritual technology based on the time-tested Vedic model to achieve the highest potential in personal and professional relationships.

(3) VAST will use Vedic solutions for resolving contemporary issues.

(4) VAST will continuously improve training standards to create leaders for tomorrow.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this auspicious day of the 107th anniversary of your divine appearance, we at VAST are begging for your blessings in this humble endeavor to serve you and instill Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the intelligentsia around the world. Lord Kṛṣṇa says in the *Bhagavad-gītā* (3.21):

yad yad ācarati śreṣṭhas tat tad evetaro janaḥ  
sa yat pramāṇaṁ kurute lokas tad anuvartate

“Whatever action a great man performs, common men follow. And whatever standards he sets by exemplary acts, all the world pursues.”

It is only by your causeless mercy that we can attain our goal. We hope and pray that, one day, we can make you proud of us.

*Your servants at the Vedic Academy of Spiritual Technology.*

## Vrajabhūmi School and Āśrama

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances.

We thank you for giving us the wonderful shelter of your lotus feet, and we pray that we will remain under that shelter for all eternity. We pray that the strength of the *saṅkīrtana* continues to increase in this troubled Age of Kali, and we know that without your *śakti* nothing of this will be possible, nor will we even have the strength to sing the holy names.

To prevent illusion from infecting our minds, we depend fully on your transcendental instructions. Only you can relieve our doubts and fears. Only if your transcendental books are increasingly read can this lost society (this so-called material civilization) become free from degradation and decadence.

We pray to you that ISKCON, your spiritual body, become more and more adored and served by mature and sincere devotees, and that it can remain for the next ten thousand years, giving people a concrete opportunity for escaping from material imprisonment.

We pray that you bless all the preaching programs spread all around the world by your devotees, and we especially pray that you bless our (your) humble Vrajabhūmi School and Āśrama project.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, we thank you for giving us *kṛṣṇa-bhakti* in a simple and refined way in your books, in your preaching, and in your life. We have no doubt that you are the personification of the whole *paramparā,* and that you desire to satisfy the wish of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta and save us. All your life has been an example of how to develop love for Kṛṣṇa. For your infinite mercy, we are eternally grateful and deeply in debt.

*Your eternal servants at the Vrajabhūmi School and Āśrama, in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil.*  
(written by Jayādvaita dāsa)

## Vṛndāvana Institute for Higher Education

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

*vayaṁ tu na vitṛpyāma uttama-śloka-vikrame*  
*yac-chṛṇvatāṁ rasa-jñānāṁ svādu svādu pade pade*

**“We never tire of hearing the transcendental pastimes of the Personality of Godhead, who is glorified by hymns and prayers. Those who have developed a taste for transcendental relationships with Him relish hearing of His pastimes at every moment. (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.1.19)**

On this most auspicious day of your merciful appearance among us, we repeatedly thank you for giving us the unmatched jewel of the timeless process of awakening love of Kṛṣṇa. Years are passing and we are not getting tired. We are eager to continue on, being convinced by direct experience that we are on the right path.

This past year was special for the VIHE, for we entered the deep ocean of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, *purāṇaṁ brahma-sammitam*. Thirty-five devotees from fifteen different countries lived in Vṛndāvana *dhāma* for four and a half months and underwent the first semester of the second level of śāstric study you designed—Bhakti-vaibhava. For practical purposes, we spread this course, including the study of the first six cantos of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* over four years. The mood set by our teachers, His Grace Bhūrijana dāsa and Her Grace Nārāyaṇī-devī dāsī, was that of inspiring sincerity and clearing the hearts from misgivings so Kṛṣṇa may be pleased to take His seat in the midst of our assembly. We savored the First and Second Canto through daily hearing, as well as by discussing the topics and their application in small groups. On the first test day all the students spontaneously broke into a half-hour dancing *kīrtana* before getting at their pens. We experienced *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam As It Is,* alive with powerful influence.

Change of heart is what we are after at the VIHE, for it is the purpose of śāstric study performed along with the others *aṅgas* of the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness—*tad-vāg-visargo janatāgha-viplavo*. Seeing the radiant faces of the students as they progress through their studies is our reward for our service. Supporting them as they go through the “real tests” they are faced with during their intense association with the *śāstra,* the *dhāma,* and each other is our privilege.

Along with the Bhakti-vaibhava course, we also held our 11th Bhakti-śāstrī course and provided a variety of classes during Kārttika. Our second retreat program at the foot of Śrī Govardhana was well attended and warmly praised by those who took part.

This year brought change and renewal. We moved out of the Gurukula building, where we’d been operating ever since our start in 1987. We are now located in a quiet, beautiful house next to your garden at the ISKCON *gośālā*. The surrounding atmosphere is ideal and most enlivening, with cows, bulls, and peacocks peacefully going about. We are looking forward to developing our service from this new paradigm. Your room, along with three classrooms, two offices, and a guest room, will be ready by June so we can hold the up-coming VIHE semesters there.

Our working team, enriched with two additional members, is enthusiastically planning and organizing. This year will see the launch of the Bhakti-vaibhava correspondence course, which will follow the path set by the already existing Bhakti-śāstrī correspondence course.

“We never tire of hearing the transcendental pastimes of the Personality of Godhead, who is glorified by hymns and prayers. Those who have developed a taste for transcendental relationships with Him relish hearing of His pastimes at every moment.” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.1.19)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, kindly let us go on and on with this receiving and transmitting of the transcendental sound vibration. We aim to deepen devotees’ understanding, realization, and appreciation of your teachings and movement and to preserve and disseminate standards you set for acting in Kṛṣṇa consciousness so the Kṛṣṇa conscious culture and values you gave us may pass on to successive generations. We are your eternal servants and wish only to be engaged in the fulfillment of your desires. Kindly give us the intelligence to develop this project of Kṛṣṇa conscious higher education so it is practical for the devotees and increasingly pleasing to Your Divine Grace. May we always be blessed with your association.

*yo nityadā jīvati daivikīṣu*  
*nijāsu śikṣāsu tu sānugāmī*  
*kṛpāmbudhir nanda-sutāśritātmā*  
*taṁ kīrtayāmaḥ prabhupādadeva*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is like an ocean of mercy, and who has completely taken shelter of Lord Kṛṣṇa, the transcendental son of Nanda Mahārāja, with his body, mind, words, and self. Śrīla Prabhupāda lives forever by his divine instructions, and his sincere followers live with him.” (*****Śrīla Prabhupādadevāṣṭaka*** **8, by Kuśakratha dāsa)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

*Your servants at the VIHE.*  
(written by Praśānta-devī dāsī)
